# M10 plan: server-sent events

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** An operation whose success is `text/event-stream` becomes a generated method that returns `Future<Result<Stream<SseItem<T>>, E>>` with typed event data, or a sealed union of the events keyed by their SSE name, a second `...Stream` method when the operation also offers JSON, a stream that reconnects with `Last-Event-ID` when the call or the operation asks for it, and an `sse` override for what the spec does not say (R12.1, R12.2, R12.4, R12.5).

**Architecture:** The runtime gets reconnection: `SseOptions` on `CallOptions`, an `SseReconnection` policy argument on `openEvents`, an `SseReconnector` that emits `SseReconnecting(attempt, delay, cause)` and reopens with `Last-Event-ID` through an injectable `wait`, after `DioRequestSender` is split so it has room (task 1). The generator first moves code out of its allowlisted IR files (task 2), then `MethodBuilder` gives one or two methods per operation and `EventStreams`/`EventData` put the event data type and the reconnection policy into `OperationIr.events` (task 3); the emitter writes `openEvents` calls from the IR only (task 4), with goldens, the analyzer gate and real calls through a fake adapter. `EventData` reads an `itemSchema` `oneOf` of named events into an `EventUnionIr`, which `EventUnionNames` names after the models and `EventUnionEmitter` writes as a sealed class with `fromEvent` (task 5, no `SchemaMapper` change); the `sse` override writes its schemas into the spec as edits and gives the IR its generation fields (task 6); the fuzz found two defects (task 7); task 8 closes the roadmap rows.

**Tech Stack:** Dart `>=3.10.0 <4.0.0`, pure Dart; runtime dependencies stay `dio` and `meta`; tests on `package:test`; browser runs with `dart test -p chrome` and `--compiler dart2wasm`; the analyzer gate runs `dart analyze --fatal-infos` in a subprocess on a generated package.

**Spec:** [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-13-m10-sse-design.md](../specs/2026-10-13-m10-sse-design.md), decisions 1-9 (binding). Requirements: R12.1, R12.2, R12.4, R12.5 ([requirements.md](../../requirements.md)); R12.6 is out of scope. Earlier designs: [M7 runtime](../specs/2026-10-12-m7-runtime-design.md) (decisions 16-18), [M9 operations](../specs/2026-10-13-m9-operations-design.md), [M4 overrides](../specs/2026-10-12-m4-overrides-design.md) (the deferred `sse` field). Carried over: [roadmap](../../roadmap.md), rows with M10 and "Parked from the M9 reviews". Code rules and size limits: [CLAUDE.md](../../../CLAUDE.md), [code-quality-review.md](../../code-quality-review.md).

## Global Constraints

- SDK `>=3.10.0 <4.0.0`; both packages pure Dart; generated code is Dart 3.10. Primary constructors (3.13) are not used.
- Dart 3.10 is used actively: dot shorthands, null-aware elements, switch expressions and patterns, records, class modifiers. In tests the arguments of `expect(...)` use full names.
- OOP, no top-level functions except `main` in `bin/` and tests. Components that report take a `DiagnosticBag` or an `IrContext`.
- The IR is the emitter's only input: the emitter never reads the spec; what it lacks is added to the IR (task 3 adds `OperationIr.events`).
- Nothing is guessed silently: every fallback is a diagnostic with a code and a JSON pointer.
- Deterministic output: the same spec and config give byte-identical files.
- Every spec string is escaped at every sink: literals through `DartCode.string`, doc comments through `DartCode.docLines`, identifiers only from the IR.
- Generated code is clean under `package:lints/recommended` plus the `flutter_lints` rules, with no `ignore_for_file`.
- The runtime depends on `dio` and `meta` only and works on the VM, dart2js and dart2wasm; new runtime code runs in the browser too (`dart test -p chrome`, also `--compiler dart2wasm`).
- Generated code never changes the user's Dio (R10.1); the stream is sent through the same user Dio (R12.4).
- The stream never emits errors: data problems are `SseDecodeError` items, the end is `SseDisconnected`, reconnection is `SseReconnecting` (spec decision 4). Tests never sleep: delays go through `SseOptions.wait`.
- Every new code is in `DiagnosticCode` with a doc comment, a kebab-case slug and a default level, in the 6xx media-type range (spec decision 9).
- Fixtures are invented and small. The company's private spec is never used. The public corpus is for runs only (outputs in the scratchpad).
- Files that another session refactors (on `main` since `fecc57c` they are facades over collaborator files) are not edited: `lib/src/ir/schema_mapper.dart`, `lib/src/ir/schema_shapes.dart`, `lib/src/generate/output_writer.dart`, their tests, `test/check/spec_checker_test.dart`, `test/cli/cli_test.dart`, `test/ir/schema_mapper*_test.dart`, `test/ir/union_subsumed_test.dart`, `test/load/spec_loader_test.dart`. M10 calls only `SchemaMapper.typeOf`/`typeOfComponent` and public `SchemaShapes` methods; new tests go to new files.
- Size limits (CLAUDE.md, "Code quality rules"): a `lib/` file has at most 300 lines, a test file 500, a method 40; the ratchet tests `packages/*/test/quality/size_limits_test.dart` pass after every task. No allowlisted unit grows in this plan: room is made by moving code out first, in `refactor(...)` commits that change no generated byte (table "Size ratchet changes").
- `dart format lib test bin` (runtime: `lib test`) changes 0 files on a second run; `dart analyze packages` from the root prints `No issues found!`; no `// ignore:`.
- Tests never touch the network (`dart pub get --offline` in subprocesses); temp files only through `test/helpers/temp_dir.dart`.
- `dart test` and `dart format` run from the package directory; `dart pub get`, `dart analyze packages`, `git apply` and `git` from the root. New files are created from their blocks in full; diffs are applied with `git apply` from the root.
- Every commit is a Conventional Commit and ends with the `Co-Authored-By` line of the model that does the task (the examples use `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`).
- Documents are in English; `README.ua.md` is the intentional Ukrainian translation of `README.md` and is kept in step with it.

## Decisions taken

The spec's decisions are binding. These are the technical choices it leaves to the plan; choices users see are under "Open questions", each with the default this plan implements.

1. **Reconnection policy as an argument, not a field.** The generated method passes `reconnection: .off` (a method other than GET that does not enable it) or `.on` (enabled for the operation) to `openEvents`; `.onRequest` (GET) is the default and is not written. `SseReconnection.reconnects(SseOptions)` decides per call: `off` ignores the call, `onRequest` needs `reconnect: true`, `on` needs no `false`. The doc comment of every such method says in one sentence that it does not reconnect unless the operation's `sse.reconnect` override enables it; the runtime ignores a per-call request by design, as the doc of `SseOptions.reconnect` says. A diagnostic only comes when reconnection was asked for (user decision): TOA665 (info), once per non-GET event-stream operation that the override does not enable, when the config's `sse.reconnect` is on (in `defaults` or the client); its message says the config applies to GET only and its fix shows the override `operations: {<op>: {sse: {reconnect: true}}}`. Without a reconnect request there is no diagnostic.
2. **Delays.** The first delay is the server's last `retry`, else `SseOptions.delay` (1 s); each further one doubles up to `maxDelay` (30 s); a server `retry` longer than `maxDelay` is kept as it is (the server asked for it). `maxAttempts` (5) counts attempts in a row without an event: an event or a decode error on a new connection starts the count again, so a server that accepts and closes at once still stops after 5.
3. **What ends a reconnecting stream.** A cancel (the listener, or `call.cancelToken`, also during a wait), the attempts running out (`SseDisconnected(cause: <last error>)`), an attempt the server answers with a status that is not a stream (`SseDisconnected(cause: <the Failure>)`: `HttpError`, `UndocumentedStatus`, `DecodeError`) and 204 No Content (WHATWG: stop reconnecting; also on the first connection). A transport failure of an attempt is the next attempt.
4. **The last event id carries over.** `SseDecoder` and `SseItemDecoder` take the last event id and `retry` a stream starts from, so `SseEvent.id` after a reconnect is the id of the connection before, as an `EventSource` keeps it.
5. **`DioRequestSender` is split before it grows** (task 1, a refactor commit): `DioCall` sends the request, `DioResults` maps responses and failures; `openEvents` keeps the web check and wraps `_open` (one connection) in `SseReconnector`.
6. **Event data type (spec decision 1).** `EventData` reads `itemSchema.properties.data`: a `contentSchema` with a JSON `contentMediaType` is `T`; a `data` that is a plain string (no `contentMediaType`) is text, `String`; anything else is text with TOA666 (from task 5 an `itemSchema` `oneOf` of named events is a union). `schema` is the JSON of the data: an array takes its `items` (TOA664), a schema whose type is a plain `String` is text (not a JSON string), no schema is text. Inline data schemas are named `<Method>Event`.
7. **One method or two (spec decision 5).** `EventStreams.shapeOf` looks at the 2xx responses: no event stream gives the M9 method; every event-stream success alone gives one stream method named as the operation; every event-stream success next to another body gives the M9 method plus `<name><stream_suffix>` (TOA663), claimed in the group's method scope after every other method (TOA551 with `2` on a collision); any other mix (one success only a stream, another only JSON) is TOA654, not generated. A bodyless 2xx joins the stream statuses (its body is empty: the stream is just `SseDisconnected`).
8. **The stream method in the IR.** `OperationIr.events` (`EventStreamIr`: `data`, null for text, and `reconnection`); its event-stream successes are `String` responses (as text) and `ApiNames` gives it no sealed success type; its decoder has error arms only, since `openEvents` opens the stream for `streamStatuses` before the decoder is asked. Event-stream successes whose data types differ are TOA654.
9. **Config.** `naming.stream_suffix` (default `Stream`; letters and digits from an uppercase letter or a digit, task 7) and `sse.reconnect` (GET event streams reconnect unless the call says no) are read by `OperationSettingsReader`, which also takes `naming.operation_prefix` out of the allowlisted `config_loader.dart`; both are in `GenerationConfig.key`.

10. **The union is not a mapper declaration (task 5).** `EventUnionIr` hangs off `EventStreamIr`; its variants' data types come from `SchemaMapper.typeOf`, its names from `EventUnionNames` (through `TypeClaims`, after the status types), its code from `EventUnionEmitter` into the group file. `schema_mapper.dart` and `schema_shapes.dart` are not touched.
11. **`data` and `events` of the override are spec edits (task 6).** They become the `schema` of the operation's own event-stream media types (an object of the events for `events`) and `itemSchema` is removed, so every later stage, the normalizer included, sees one spec in any OpenAPI version, and an inline schema is read by the parser like any other; `EventData` takes the variants from that object in the override's order.
12. **`unknown: ignore` (task 6)** drops an event in the runtime: the generated `fromEvent` throws `SseEventIgnored` and `SseItemDecoder` emits nothing for it; the union then has no unknown variant, so a `switch` names only the events the spec gives. Its `id` still moves the last event id (controller ruling, WHATWG sets it for any dispatched event): `SseItemDecoder` records the id and `retry` of every message and puts them on the `SseDisconnected` it ends with (`lastEventId`, `retry`), and `SseReconnector` takes them from there, so a reconnection sends the id of a dropped last event. `SseItemDecoder.bind` (allowlisted at 52 lines) moves into a private `_Decoding` class to make room.

## New diagnostic codes

| Code | Slug | Level | When | Task |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| TOA654 | `event-stream-not-generated` | warning | Narrowed: event-stream successes with different data types, or a success that is only an event stream next to one with another body; the operation is not generated | 3 |
| TOA663 | `event-stream-method-added` | info | A success offers JSON (or another body) and `text/event-stream`: a second method returns the events | 3 |
| TOA664 | `event-data-array-items` | info | An event stream's `schema` is an array: each event's data is one item | 3 |
| TOA665 | `event-stream-reconnect-off` | info | The config's `sse.reconnect` is on, but a non-GET event-stream operation does not reconnect (the config applies to GET); the fix shows the override that enables it. Reported once per such operation, and only when the config asks | 3 |
| TOA666 | `event-item-schema-unread` | warning | An `itemSchema` does not say what an event's data is: read as text | 3 |
| TOA667 | `event-name-duplicate` | warning | Two branches of an `itemSchema` union fix the same event name: the later one is left out | 5 |
| TOA668 | `event-name-unrepresentable` | info | An event name has no ASCII letter or digit: its variant is named by its place | 5 |
| TOA669 | `sse-override-not-applied` | warning | An `sse` override finds no `text/event-stream` 2xx response written in its operation | 6 |

## New config keys

```yaml
# typed_openapi.yaml, in defaults or a client
naming:
  stream_suffix: Stream   # the second method of an operation that offers JSON and events: chatStream
sse:
  reconnect: false        # true: GET event streams reconnect unless a call says no
```

An invalid value is TOA902 and the default is used; an unknown key under `sse` is TOA903.

```yaml
# typed_openapi.overrides.yaml (task 6)
operations:
  createChat:
    sse:
      data: ChatChunk                          # or events: {delta: ChatDelta, done: null}
      unknown: ignore                          # keep (default) | ignore
      reconnect: true                          # also for a POST
      method: only                             # stream (default) | only | none
```

## Generated shape

```dart
/// Returns the events of `text/event-stream` as they arrive; the stream ends
/// with `SseDisconnected`. It reconnects with `Last-Event-ID` when `call.sse`
/// asks for it.
///
/// `GET /threads/{threadId}/events` (`watchThread`).
Future<Result<Stream<SseItem<Thread>>, Problem>> watchThread({
  required String threadId,
  CallOptions? call,
}) => _sender.openEvents(
  OperationRequest(
    _watchThread,
    path: [PathParameter('threadId', threadId)],
    streamStatuses: {200, 204},
  ),
  (m) => Thread.fromJson(JsonReader.asObject(m.json)),
  _decodeWatchThread,
  call: call,
);
```

A caller reconnects with `call: CallOptions(sse: SseOptions(reconnect: true))`; a POST stream gets `reconnection: .off` and ignores that.

## Interfaces between tasks

```dart
// runtime (task 1)
final class SseOptions { const SseOptions({bool? reconnect, Duration delay = const Duration(seconds: 1),
  Duration maxDelay = const Duration(seconds: 30), int maxAttempts = 5, Future<void> Function(Duration)? wait});
  Duration delayBefore(int attempt, Duration? retry); }
enum SseReconnection { off, onRequest, on; bool reconnects(SseOptions options); }
final class SseReconnecting<T> extends SseItem<T> { const SseReconnecting(int attempt, Duration delay, {Object? cause, StackTrace? stackTrace}); }
typedef SseResume = ({String lastEventId, Duration? retry});
final class SseReconnector<T> extends StreamTransformerBase<SseItem<T>, SseItem<T>> {
  const SseReconnector(SseOptions options, Future<Result<Stream<SseItem<T>>, Object?>> Function(SseResume) reopen, {Future<DioException>? cancelled}); }
CallOptions({..., SseOptions? sse});
const SseDecoder({String lastEventId = '', Duration? retry});
const SseItemDecoder(decode, {onClose, String lastEventId = '', Duration? retry});
Future<Result<Stream<SseItem<T>>, E>> DioRequestSender.openEvents<T, E>(request, decodeEvent, decodeError, {CallOptions? call, SseReconnection reconnection = .onRequest});

// generator (tasks 2, 3)
final class OperationIr { ..., EventStreamIr? events; }          // operation_ir.dart, exported by ir.dart
enum StreamReconnection { off, onRequest, on }
final class EventStreamIr { const EventStreamIr({DartType? data, StreamReconnection reconnection = .onRequest}); }
List<OperationIr> OperationBuilder.build(SpecOperation entry, String name, NameScope methods);
final class MethodBuilder { MethodBuilder(IrContext, SchemaMapper); List<OperationIr> of(SpecOperation, String name, List<ParameterIr>, NameScope methods); }
enum StreamShape { none, only, both, mixed }
final class EventStreams { EventStreams(IrContext, SchemaMapper); StreamShape shapeOf(Operation);
  String methodName(SpecOperation, String name, NameScope methods); EventStreamIr? events(SpecOperation, List<PickedResponse>, String prefix); }
final class EventData { const EventData(IrContext, SchemaMapper); DartType? of(MediaType?, SpecLocation, NameHint); }
List<PickedResponse> ResponseBuilder.pick(Operation, {bool events = false, bool hasStreamMethod = false});
(String?, MediaType?) MediaTypePicker.pick(content, location, what, {bool hasStreamMethod = false});
GenerationConfig({..., String streamSuffix = 'Stream', bool streamReconnect = false});

// union (task 5)
final class EventUnionIr { const EventUnionIr(List<EventVariantIr> variants, {required SpecLocation location, String name = '',
  String unknownName = '', bool keepUnknown = true, bool unknownSensitive = false});
  EventUnionIr named(String name, String unknownName, List<EventVariantIr> variants); EventUnionIr keeping({required bool keepUnknown}); }  // keeping: task 6
final class EventVariantIr { const EventVariantIr(String event, {String name = '', bool hasData = true, DartType? data, bool sensitive = false});
  EventVariantIr named(String name); }
EventStreamIr({DartType? data, EventUnionIr? union, StreamReconnection reconnection = .onRequest}); EventStreamIr withUnion(EventUnionIr union);
typedef EventShape = ({DartType? data, EventUnionIr? union});
EventShape EventData.of(MediaType? media, SpecLocation location, String prefix, {SseOverride? sse});   // sse: task 6

final class EventUnionEmitter { EventUnionEmitter(TypeCodec); void write(DartWriter, EventUnionIr, OperationIr, GroupIr); }
bool ResponseSecrets.carriesSecret(Schema schema);

// override (task 6)
enum SseMethod { stream, only, none }
final class SseOverride { const SseOverride({required JsonPointer pointer, Map<String, Object?>? data,
  Map<String, Map<String, Object?>?>? events, bool? keepUnknown, bool? reconnect, SseMethod? method});
  static Map<String, Object?> component(String name); }
OperationOverride({..., SseOverride? sse});
final class SseOverrideReader { const SseOverrideReader(OverrideFieldReader); SseOverride? read(Map<String, Object?> entry, JsonPointer at); }
final class SseOverrideApplier { const SseOverrideApplier(OverrideEditContext); Iterable<OverrideEdit> edits(OperationTarget target); }
enum StreamShape { none, only, both, mixed, excluded }
final class SseEventIgnored implements Exception { const SseEventIgnored(); }   // runtime
SseDisconnected({Object? cause, StackTrace? stackTrace, String? lastEventId, Duration? retry});   // runtime, lastEventId and retry: task 6
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: M7: `DioRequestSender.openEvents`, `SseItemDecoder`, `SseDecoder`, `SseItem`, `CallOptions`; `test/helpers/fake_adapter.dart`.
- Produces: `SseOptions`, `SseReconnection`, `SseReconnecting`, `SseReconnector`, `SseResume` (exported by `typed_openapi_runtime.dart`), `CallOptions.sse`, `openEvents(..., reconnection:)`; internal `DioCall`, `DioResults`.

Spec decisions 3 and 4 (R12.5). `dio_request_sender.dart` is allowlisted at 378 lines and reconnection needs about 50 more, so step 1 moves the Dio call (`DioCall`: the options, the headers, the `//` guard of the query) and the mapping to `Result` (`DioResults`: `raw`, `decode`, `failure`) into files of their own; the file drops to 233 lines and leaves the allowlist, and so does `_request` (42 lines), which becomes `DioCall.send` and `DioCall._options`. Then the feature: `openEvents` opens the first connection through `_open`, and when the policy and `call.sse` say so wraps its stream in `SseReconnector`, which reopens through the same `_open` with the last event id. The new item `SseReconnecting` breaks exhaustive switches over `SseItem` (the runtime's own tests get a case), which is intended: the spec makes reconnection visible in the stream. The reconnection tests run on the VM and in the browser (they use only `FakeAdapter`); no test sleeps, `SseOptions.wait` records the delays.

- [ ] **Step 1: Move the Dio call and the result mapping out of `DioRequestSender` (refactor)**

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/lib/src/dio/dio_call.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';

import '../request/operation_info.dart';
import '../request/operation_request.dart';
import 'call_options.dart';
import 'dio_body.dart';

/// Sends the request of an [OperationRequest] through the user's [Dio]:
/// the method, the finished path and query, the headers, the body and how
/// Dio reads the response. The Dio instance is never changed.
final class DioCall {
  /// Creates the call through [dio].
  const DioCall(this.dio);

  /// The user's Dio instance.
  final Dio dio;

  /// Sends [request] with [call]: for an event stream ([events]) as a
  /// stream with `Accept: text/event-stream`, else read as
  /// [OperationRequest.responseKind] says. [cancelToken] wins over the
  /// call's.
  Future<Response<Object?>> send(
    OperationRequest request,
    CallOptions? call, {
    bool events = false,
    CancelToken? cancelToken,
  }) {
    final options = _options(request, call, events: events);
    // The type argument picks how Dio reads the body: Dio forces JSON for
    // any type but String, List<int> with bytes or ResponseBody with stream.
    Future<Response<R>> send<R>() => dio.request<R>(
      _guardQuery(request.resolvePath()),
      data: const DioBody().data(request),
      options: options,
      cancelToken: cancelToken ?? call?.cancelToken,
      onSendProgress: call?.onSendProgress,
      onReceiveProgress: call?.onReceiveProgress,
    );
    if (events) return send<ResponseBody>();
    return switch (request.responseKind) {
      .json => send<Object?>(),
      .text => send<String>(),
      .bytes => send<List<int>>(),
    };
  }

  Options _options(
    OperationRequest request,
    CallOptions? call, {
    required bool events,
  }) => Options(
    method: request.operation.method,
    headers: {
      if (events) 'Accept': 'text/event-stream',
      ...request.resolveHeaders(),
      for (final MapEntry(:key, :value) in call?.headers.entries ?? const [])
        key: ?value,
      ...const DioBody().headers(request.body),
    },
    extra: {...?call?.extra, OperationInfo.extraKey: request.operation},
    contentType: const DioBody().contentType(request),
    responseType: events
        ? .stream
        : switch (request.responseKind) {
            .json => .json,
            .text => .plain,
            .bytes => .bytes,
          },
  );

  /// Dio joins the base URL and a relative path and then collapses every
  /// `//` of the whole string, query included (`RequestOptions.uri`), which
  /// would change an `allowReserved` value such as `next=/a//b`. When the
  /// query holds `//`, the base URL is joined here, collapsing `//` in the
  /// path part only, and Dio leaves the absolute URL alone. Otherwise the
  /// relative path goes to Dio, so interceptors that change `baseUrl` keep
  /// working; interceptors see the finished URI either way.
  String _guardQuery(String path) {
    final queryStart = path.indexOf('?');
    final base = dio.options.baseUrl;
    if (queryStart < 0 ||
        !path.substring(queryStart).contains('//') ||
        !_absolute.hasMatch(base)) {
      return path;
    }
    final joined = (base + path.substring(0, queryStart)).split(':/');
    final target = joined.length == 2
        ? '${joined[0]}:/${joined[1].replaceAll('//', '/')}'
        : joined.join(':/');
    return target + path.substring(queryStart);
  }

  static final RegExp _absolute = RegExp('^https?:');
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/lib/src/dio/dio_results.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';

import '../json/json_decode_exception.dart';
import '../request/raw_response.dart';
import '../result/result.dart';

/// Turns what Dio gives `DioRequestSender` (a response, or a
/// [DioException] without one) into a [Result].
final class DioResults {
  /// Creates the mapping; [isWeb] tells whether the program runs in a
  /// browser (see `DioRequestSender.isWeb`).
  const DioResults({required this.isWeb});

  /// Whether the program runs in a browser.
  final bool isWeb;

  /// A response without a status (only a faulty interceptor builds one)
  /// gets status 0, which no operation documents.
  static RawResponse raw(Response<Object?> response, Object? body) =>
      RawResponse(
        statusCode: response.statusCode ?? 0,
        body: body,
        headers: response.headers.map,
      );

  /// [raw] decoded with [decode]; a [JsonDecodeException] or a
  /// [FormatException] it throws is a [DecodeError].
  static Result<T, E> decode<T, E>(
    RawResponse raw,
    Result<T, E> Function(RawResponse response) decode,
  ) {
    try {
      return decode(raw);
    } on JsonDecodeException catch (e) {
      return _decodeError(e, raw);
    } on FormatException catch (e) {
      // A user converter (R6.8) or `Date.parse` rejecting server data.
      return _decodeError(JsonDecodeException.fromFormatException(e), raw);
    }
  }

  static DecodeError<T, E> _decodeError<T, E>(
    JsonDecodeException error,
    RawResponse raw,
  ) => DecodeError(
    error,
    statusCode: raw.statusCode,
    rawBody: raw.body,
    headers: raw.headers,
  );

  /// A failure without a response.
  ///
  /// - Cancellation is [Cancelled].
  /// - An `unknown` failure whose cause is an [Error] (a `TypeError` or
  ///   `StateError` thrown in a user interceptor or adapter, which Dio
  ///   wraps) is a bug, not a transport problem: it is rethrown with its
  ///   original stack trace.
  /// - Dio drops the response when its JSON parser fails (`unknown` with a
  ///   [FormatException]), so the result has no status. That is a decode
  ///   error when the request reads JSON and the exception reports the bytes
  ///   of the body (what Dio's default transformer does on the VM and
  ///   dart2wasm): the raw body is that text (for a body Dio read in chunks,
  ///   only the chunk that failed). On the web ([isWeb]) every such
  ///   [FormatException] counts: dart2js reports the browser's `SyntaxError`
  ///   text and no source, so the raw body is the source when it is text and
  ///   `null` otherwise; a [FormatException] from an interceptor of a JSON
  ///   request is a decode error there too, because the failures cannot be
  ///   told apart. On the VM a [FormatException] from anywhere else
  ///   (`Uri.parse` in an interceptor, a custom transformer that reports
  ///   text) stays a [TransportError]; a request that does not read JSON
  ///   never gets a decode error here.
  Failure<T, E> failure<T, E>(DioException e, StackTrace stackTrace) {
    switch (e) {
      case DioException(type: .cancel):
        return Cancelled(e, stackTrace);
      case DioException(type: .unknown, error: final Error cause):
        Error.throwWithStackTrace(cause, e.stackTrace);
      case DioException(
            type: .unknown,
            response: null,
            requestOptions: RequestOptions(responseType: .json),
            error: FormatException(:final source),
          )
          when source is List<int> || isWeb:
        return DecodeError(
          JsonDecodeException(
            expected: 'a JSON body',
            actual: 'text that is not JSON',
          ),
          rawBody: switch (source) {
            final List<int> bytes => utf8.decode(bytes, allowMalformed: true),
            final String text => text,
            _ => null,
          },
        );
      default:
        return TransportError(e, stackTrace);
    }
  }
}
````

- [ ] **Step 2: Verify the refactor and commit it**

From `packages/typed_openapi_runtime`: `dart format lib test` (a second run changes 0 files) and `dart test` (expected `+228: All tests passed!`, the same as before). From the root: `dart analyze packages` prints `No issues found!`.

```bash
git add -A
git commit -m "refactor(typed_openapi_runtime): move the Dio call and result mapping out of DioRequestSender

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the failing tests**

The new test file pins the delays, the policy matrix, `Last-Event-ID`, the server's `retry`, the id that carries over, the backoff cap and the attempt limit, a POST that ignores the call, an error status on an attempt, and cancel during the wait, by the listener during the wait and during the stream. The other test files get a case for the new item.

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/test/sse/sse_reconnect_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_runtime/typed_openapi_runtime.dart';

import '../helpers/fake_adapter.dart';

/// An event-stream response with [text] that then closes.
ResponseBody _stream(String text, [int status = 200]) => ResponseBody(
  Stream.value(Uint8List.fromList(utf8.encode(text))),
  status,
  headers: {
    Headers.contentTypeHeader: ['text/event-stream'],
  },
);

/// A connection that fails before any response.
ResponseBody _refused(RequestOptions options) => throw DioException(
  requestOptions: options,
  type: DioExceptionType.connectionError,
  message: 'refused',
);

/// Answers the requests in order with [answers]; the last one repeats.
FakeAdapter _adapter(List<ResponseBody Function(RequestOptions)> answers) {
  var next = 0;
  return FakeAdapter(
    (options) => answers[next < answers.length - 1 ? next++ : next](options),
  );
}

void main() {
  const watch = OperationRequest(
    OperationInfo(operationId: 'watch', method: 'GET', pathTemplate: '/w'),
    streamStatuses: {200, 204},
  );

  Result<Stream<SseItem<String>>, String> decodeError(RawResponse r) =>
      switch (r.statusCode) {
        404 => r.error(r.text),
        _ => r.undocumented(),
      };

  String describe(SseItem<String> item) => switch (item) {
    SseEvent(:final data, :final id) => 'event $data #$id',
    SseDecodeError() => 'decode error',
    SseReconnecting(:final attempt, :final delay) =>
      'reconnecting $attempt after ${delay.inMilliseconds}ms',
    SseDisconnected(cause: DioException(:final type)) => 'end ${type.name}',
    SseDisconnected(cause: HttpError(:final statusCode)) =>
      'end http $statusCode',
    SseDisconnected(:final cause) => 'end ${cause ?? 'closed'}',
  };

  /// The items of a [watch] stream opened through [adapter].
  Future<List<String>> items(
    FakeAdapter adapter, {
    SseReconnection reconnection = SseReconnection.onRequest,
    SseOptions? sse,
    List<Duration>? waits,
  }) async {
    final options = SseOptions(
      reconnect: sse?.reconnect,
      maxAttempts: sse?.maxAttempts ?? 5,
      maxDelay: sse?.maxDelay ?? const Duration(seconds: 30),
      wait: (delay) async => waits?.add(delay),
    );
    final result = await DioRequestSender(Dio()..httpClientAdapter = adapter)
        .openEvents(
          watch,
          (m) => m.data,
          decodeError,
          call: CallOptions(sse: options),
          reconnection: reconnection,
        );
    final stream = (result as Success<Stream<SseItem<String>>, String>).data;
    return (await stream.toList()).map(describe).toList();
  }

  group('delays', () {
    test('double from 1 s up to 30 s without a server retry', () {
      const options = SseOptions();
      expect(
        [for (var i = 1; i <= 7; i++) options.delayBefore(i, null)],
        [
          for (final s in [1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 30, 30]) Duration(seconds: s),
        ],
      );
    });

    test('start from the server retry', () {
      const options = SseOptions();
      const retry = Duration(milliseconds: 2500);
      expect(options.delayBefore(1, retry), retry);
      expect(options.delayBefore(2, retry), const Duration(seconds: 5));
      expect(options.delayBefore(5, retry), const Duration(seconds: 30));
    });

    test('a server retry over the cap is kept as it is', () {
      const options = SseOptions();
      const retry = Duration(minutes: 2);
      expect(options.delayBefore(3, retry), retry);
    });
  });

  test('a GET reconnects only when the call asks, a POST never', () {
    const asks = SseOptions(reconnect: true);
    const refuses = SseOptions(reconnect: false);
    const none = SseOptions();
    expect(
      [
        for (final policy in SseReconnection.values)
          [
            for (final options in [none, asks, refuses])
              policy.reconnects(options),
          ],
      ],
      [
        [false, false, false],
        [false, true, false],
        [true, true, false],
      ],
    );
  });

  test('without reconnection the stream ends when the server closes', () async {
    final adapter = _adapter([(_) => _stream('data: a\n\n')]);
    expect(await items(adapter), ['event a #', 'end closed']);
    expect(adapter.requests, hasLength(1));
  });

  test('a reconnect sends Last-Event-ID and waits the server retry', () async {
    final waits = <Duration>[];
    final adapter = _adapter([
      (_) => _stream('retry: 2500\nid: 7\ndata: a\n\n'),
      (_) => _stream('data: b\n\n', 204),
    ]);
    expect(
      await items(
        adapter,
        sse: const SseOptions(reconnect: true),
        waits: waits,
      ),
      ['event a #7', 'reconnecting 1 after 2500ms', 'end closed'],
    );
    expect(waits, [const Duration(milliseconds: 2500)]);
    expect(adapter.requests[0].headers['Last-Event-ID'], isNull);
    expect(adapter.requests[1].headers['Last-Event-ID'], '7');
  });

  test('the event id carries over to the next connection', () async {
    final adapter = _adapter([
      (_) => _stream('id: 7\ndata: a\n\n'),
      (_) => _stream('data: b\n\n'),
      (_) => _stream('', 204),
    ]);
    expect(
      await items(adapter, reconnection: SseReconnection.on),
      [
        'event a #7',
        'reconnecting 1 after 1000ms',
        'event b #7',
        'reconnecting 1 after 1000ms',
        'end closed',
      ],
    );
    expect(adapter.requests[2].headers['Last-Event-ID'], '7');
  });

  test(
    'failed attempts back off up to the cap and stop at the limit',
    () async {
      final waits = <Duration>[];
      final adapter = _adapter([(_) => _stream('data: a\n\n'), _refused]);
      expect(
        await items(
          adapter,
          sse: const SseOptions(
            reconnect: true,
            maxAttempts: 4,
            maxDelay: Duration(seconds: 3),
          ),
          waits: waits,
        ),
        [
          'event a #',
          'reconnecting 1 after 1000ms',
          'reconnecting 2 after 2000ms',
          'reconnecting 3 after 3000ms',
          'reconnecting 4 after 3000ms',
          'end connectionError',
        ],
      );
      expect(adapter.requests, hasLength(5));
    },
  );

  test('a POST does not reconnect even when the call asks', () async {
    final adapter = _adapter([(_) => _stream('data: a\n\n')]);
    expect(
      await items(
        adapter,
        reconnection: SseReconnection.off,
        sse: const SseOptions(reconnect: true),
      ),
      ['event a #', 'end closed'],
    );
  });

  test('an attempt answered with an error status ends the stream', () async {
    final adapter = _adapter([
      (_) => _stream('data: a\n\n'),
      (_) => ResponseBody.fromString('gone', 404),
    ]);
    expect(await items(adapter, reconnection: SseReconnection.on), [
      'event a #',
      'reconnecting 1 after 1000ms',
      'end http 404',
    ]);
  });

  test(
    'cancelling during the wait ends the stream, no request follows',
    () async {
      final token = CancelToken();
      final adapter = _adapter([(_) => _stream('data: a\n\n')]);
      final result = await DioRequestSender(Dio()..httpClientAdapter = adapter)
          .openEvents(
            watch,
            (m) => m.data,
            decodeError,
            call: CallOptions(
              cancelToken: token,
              sse: SseOptions(
                reconnect: true,
                wait: (_) => Completer<void>().future,
              ),
            ),
          );
      final stream = (result as Success<Stream<SseItem<String>>, String>).data;
      final seen = <String>[];
      final done = Completer<void>();
      stream.listen((item) {
        seen.add(describe(item));
        if (item is SseReconnecting) token.cancel();
      }, onDone: done.complete);
      await done.future;
      expect(seen, ['event a #', 'reconnecting 1 after 1000ms', 'end cancel']);
      expect(adapter.requests, hasLength(1));
    },
  );

  test('a listener that cancels during the wait stops the attempts', () async {
    final wait = Completer<void>();
    final adapter = _adapter([(_) => _stream('data: a\n\n')]);
    final result = await DioRequestSender(Dio()..httpClientAdapter = adapter)
        .openEvents(
          watch,
          (m) => m.data,
          decodeError,
          call: CallOptions(
            sse: SseOptions(reconnect: true, wait: (_) => wait.future),
          ),
        );
    final stream = (result as Success<Stream<SseItem<String>>, String>).data;
    late final StreamSubscription<SseItem<String>> subscription;
    subscription = stream.listen((item) {
      if (item is SseReconnecting) subscription.cancel();
    });
    await pumpEventQueue();
    wait.complete();
    await pumpEventQueue();
    expect(adapter.requests, hasLength(1));
  });

  test('cancelling during the stream ends it without a reconnect', () async {
    final token = CancelToken();
    final body = StreamController<Uint8List>();
    addTearDown(body.close);
    final adapter = _adapter([
      (_) => ResponseBody(
        body.stream,
        200,
        headers: {
          Headers.contentTypeHeader: ['text/event-stream'],
        },
      ),
    ]);
    final result = await DioRequestSender(Dio()..httpClientAdapter = adapter)
        .openEvents(
          watch,
          (m) => m.data,
          decodeError,
          call: CallOptions(cancelToken: token),
          reconnection: SseReconnection.on,
        );
    final stream = (result as Success<Stream<SseItem<String>>, String>).data;
    final seen = <String>[];
    final done = Completer<void>();
    stream.listen((item) {
      seen.add(describe(item));
      if (item is SseEvent) token.cancel();
    }, onDone: done.complete);
    body.add(Uint8List.fromList(utf8.encode('data: a\n\n')));
    await done.future;
    expect(seen, ['event a #', 'end cancel']);
    expect(adapter.requests, hasLength(1));
  });
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/lib/src/sse/sse_options.dart`:

````dart
/// How an event stream reconnects after its connection ends (R12.5): the
/// `sse` field of `CallOptions`.
///
/// Reconnection is off unless the call or the operation turns it on. Before
/// each attempt the stream gets an `SseReconnecting` item; a reconnect sends
/// the last event id as `Last-Event-ID`. The first delay is the server's
/// `retry`, else [delay]; each further delay doubles up to [maxDelay] (a
/// server `retry` longer than [maxDelay] is kept as it is). After
/// [maxAttempts] attempts in a row without an event, the stream ends with
/// `SseDisconnected`.
final class SseOptions {
  /// Creates the options of one call.
  const SseOptions({
    this.reconnect,
    this.delay = const Duration(seconds: 1),
    this.maxDelay = const Duration(seconds: 30),
    this.maxAttempts = 5,
    this.wait,
  });

  /// Whether the stream reconnects; null keeps the operation's setting
  /// (see [SseReconnection]). `true` has no effect on an operation whose
  /// reconnection is [SseReconnection.off] (a POST that does not enable it):
  /// repeating such a request may not be safe.
  final bool? reconnect;

  /// The first delay when the server sent no `retry`.
  final Duration delay;

  /// The longest delay a doubling reaches.
  final Duration maxDelay;

  /// How many attempts in a row may fail before the stream ends; an event
  /// on a new connection starts the count again.
  final int maxAttempts;

  /// Waits [delay] before an attempt; `Future.delayed` when null. Tests
  /// pass a function that does not sleep.
  final Future<void> Function(Duration delay)? wait;

  /// The delay before [attempt] (1 for the first), given the server's last
  /// [retry].
  Duration delayBefore(int attempt, Duration? retry) {
    final first = retry ?? delay;
    final cap = first > maxDelay ? first : maxDelay;
    var result = first;
    for (var i = 1; i < attempt && result < cap; i++) {
      result *= 2;
    }
    return result > cap ? cap : result;
  }
}

/// Whether an event-stream operation reconnects, as the generated method
/// says: by default a GET reconnects when the call asks for it, and an
/// operation that enables reconnection reconnects unless the call says no.
enum SseReconnection {
  /// Never: the operation is not GET and does not enable reconnection.
  off,

  /// When [SseOptions.reconnect] is true.
  onRequest,

  /// Unless [SseOptions.reconnect] is false.
  on;

  /// Whether a call with [options] reconnects.
  bool reconnects(SseOptions options) => switch (this) {
    off => false,
    onRequest => options.reconnect ?? false,
    on => options.reconnect ?? true,
  };
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/lib/src/sse/sse_reconnector.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';

import '../result/result.dart';
import 'sse_item.dart';
import 'sse_options.dart';

/// Where a new connection of an event stream picks up: the last event id
/// (sent as `Last-Event-ID`) and the server's last reconnection time.
typedef SseResume = ({String lastEventId, Duration? retry});

/// Keeps an event stream going across connections (R12.5).
///
/// When a connection ends ([SseDisconnected] from the server closing it or
/// from a failed connection), the stream emits [SseReconnecting], waits,
/// and calls [reopen] with the last event id. A new connection's items
/// follow; a failed attempt (a [TransportError]) is the next attempt. The
/// stream ends with [SseDisconnected] when [SseOptions.maxAttempts]
/// attempts in a row bring no event, when the request is cancelled (the
/// listener cancels, or [cancelled] completes, also during a wait), when
/// the server answers an attempt with a status that is not a stream (the
/// `Failure` is the cause), or with 204 No Content, which tells a client
/// to stop reconnecting.
final class SseReconnector<T>
    extends StreamTransformerBase<SseItem<T>, SseItem<T>> {
  /// Creates the reconnection of one call.
  const SseReconnector(this.options, this.reopen, {this.cancelled});

  /// The delays and the number of attempts.
  final SseOptions options;

  /// Opens the stream again from a [SseResume].
  final Future<Result<Stream<SseItem<T>>, Object?>> Function(SseResume resume)
  reopen;

  /// Completes when the call's cancel token is cancelled.
  final Future<DioException>? cancelled;

  @override
  Stream<SseItem<T>> bind(Stream<SseItem<T>> stream) =>
      _Reconnection<T>(this).start(stream);
}

/// The state of one reconnecting stream.
final class _Reconnection<T> {
  _Reconnection(this._owner);

  final SseReconnector<T> _owner;
  // Set in start, before anything can listen.
  late final StreamController<SseItem<T>> _out;
  StreamSubscription<SseItem<T>>? _current;
  String _lastEventId = '';
  Duration? _retry;
  int _attempt = 0;
  bool _closed = false;
  DioException? _cancel;

  Stream<SseItem<T>> start(Stream<SseItem<T>> first) {
    _owner.cancelled?.then((e) => _cancel = e);
    _out = StreamController<SseItem<T>>(
      onListen: () => _listen(first),
      onPause: () => _current?.pause(),
      onResume: () => _current?.resume(),
      onCancel: () {
        _closed = true;
        return _current?.cancel();
      },
    );
    return _out.stream;
  }

  void _listen(Stream<SseItem<T>> stream) {
    _current = stream.listen(
      _onItem,
      onError: (Object error, StackTrace stackTrace) {
        // An Error of the event decoder: a bug, forwarded as M7 does.
        _closed = true;
        _out
          ..addError(error, stackTrace)
          ..close();
      },
    );
  }

  void _onItem(SseItem<T> item) {
    switch (item) {
      case SseEvent(:final id, :final retry):
        _remember(id, retry);
        _out.add(item);
      case SseDecodeError(:final message):
        _remember(message.id, message.retry);
        _out.add(item);
      case SseReconnecting():
        _out.add(item);
      case SseDisconnected(cause: DioException(type: .cancel)):
        _end(item);
      case SseDisconnected(:final cause, :final stackTrace):
        _current = null;
        unawaited(_reconnect(cause, stackTrace));
    }
  }

  void _remember(String id, Duration? retry) {
    _lastEventId = id;
    _retry = retry;
    _attempt = 0;
  }

  Future<void> _reconnect(Object? cause, StackTrace? stackTrace) async {
    final options = _owner.options;
    if (_attempt >= options.maxAttempts) {
      return _end(SseDisconnected(cause: cause, stackTrace: stackTrace));
    }
    _attempt++;
    final delay = options.delayBefore(_attempt, _retry);
    _out.add(
      SseReconnecting(_attempt, delay, cause: cause, stackTrace: stackTrace),
    );
    await _wait(delay);
    if (_closed) return;
    if (_cancel case final cancel?) {
      return _end(
        SseDisconnected(cause: cancel, stackTrace: cancel.stackTrace),
      );
    }
    _resume(await _owner.reopen((lastEventId: _lastEventId, retry: _retry)));
  }

  /// Goes on with the [result] of an attempt.
  void _resume(Result<Stream<SseItem<T>>, Object?> result) {
    final isNoContent = switch (result) {
      Success(statusCode: 204) => true,
      _ => false,
    };
    if (_closed || isNoContent) {
      // Listening and cancelling closes the connection of the response.
      if (result case Success(:final data)) data.listen(null).cancel();
      _end(const SseDisconnected());
      return;
    }
    switch (result) {
      case Success(:final data):
        _listen(data);
      case TransportError(:final exception, :final stackTrace):
        unawaited(_reconnect(exception, stackTrace));
      case Cancelled(:final exception, :final stackTrace):
        _end(SseDisconnected(cause: exception, stackTrace: stackTrace));
      case final Failure<Stream<SseItem<T>>, Object?> failure:
        _end(SseDisconnected(cause: failure));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _wait(Duration delay) {
    final wait = (_owner.options.wait ?? Future<void>.delayed)(delay);
    final cancelled = _owner.cancelled;
    return cancelled == null ? wait : Future.any([wait, cancelled]);
  }

  void _end(SseDisconnected<T> last) {
    if (_closed) return;
    _closed = true;
    _out
      ..add(last)
      ..close();
  }
}
````

### Task 2: Room in the IR: move `OperationIr`, responses, the request body and the parameter checks out (refactor)

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/ir.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/operation_builder.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/operation_ir.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/parameter_checks.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/request_body_builder.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/response_builder.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/spec_operation.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/quality/size_limits_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: M9: `OperationBuilder`, `OperationIr`, `PickedResponse`, `MediaTypePicker`, `ResponseSecrets`, `ResponseStatuses`, `PartsBuilder`, `BodyEncoding`, `ParameterStyles`.
- Produces: `operation_ir.dart` (`OperationIr`, exported by `ir.dart`); `spec_operation.dart` (`SpecOperation`, exported by `operation_builder.dart`, so no import changes); `ResponseBuilder(IrContext, SchemaMapper)` with `pick(Operation)`, `of(List<PickedResponse>, String prefix)`, `errors(List<ResponseIr>)`; `RequestBodyBuilder(IrContext, SchemaMapper)` with `of(Operation, String method, String prefix)`; `ParameterChecks(IrContext)` with `isSendable` and `checkedStyle`.

`ir.dart` (824 lines) and `operation_builder.dart` (509) are allowlisted and must not grow, but task 3 adds a field to `OperationIr` and a second method per operation. This task only moves code: the classes and methods are copied unchanged (made public where another file calls them), `ResponseBuilder.of` gets its type switch as `_bodyType` to stay under 40 lines. `ir.dart` goes to 709 lines; `operation_builder.dart` to 269 and leaves the allowlist with `OperationBuilder._responses`. No test changes but the ratchet: generated output is byte-identical, which the petstore and pets goldens and every analyzer gate check.

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/operation_ir.dart`:

````dart
import '../model/spec_node.dart';
import 'ir.dart';

/// An operation: one method of a group.
final class OperationIr {
  /// Creates an operation.
  const OperationIr({
    required this.location,
    required this.name,
    required this.method,
    required this.path,
    required this.parameters,
    required this.responses,
    required this.errors,
    this.operationId,
    this.tags = const [],
    this.body,
    this.summary,
    this.description,
    this.deprecated = false,
    this.security = const [],
    this.successType,
    this.errorType,
    this.partsName,
  });

  /// This operation with the names of the types the API emitter declares
  /// for it, given after the declarations.
  OperationIr withTypes({
    StatusTypeIr? successType,
    StatusTypeIr? errorType,
    String? partsName,
  }) => OperationIr(
    location: location,
    name: name,
    method: method,
    path: path,
    parameters: parameters,
    responses: responses,
    errors: errors,
    operationId: operationId,
    tags: tags,
    body: body,
    summary: summary,
    description: description,
    deprecated: deprecated,
    security: security,
    successType: successType,
    errorType: errorType,
    partsName: partsName,
  );

  /// Where the Operation Object is.
  final SpecLocation location;

  /// The Dart name of the method.
  final String name;

  /// The HTTP method, upper case.
  final String method;

  /// The path template.
  final String path;

  /// `operationId`, as in the spec.
  final String? operationId;

  /// `tags`, as in the spec.
  final List<String> tags;

  /// The parameters: path, query, header, cookie, in the spec's order with
  /// the path item's first.
  final List<ParameterIr> parameters;

  /// The request body.
  final RequestBodyIr? body;

  /// The responses, by status as in the spec.
  final List<ResponseIr> responses;

  /// The type of documented errors (R11.4).
  final ErrorsIr errors;

  /// `summary`.
  final String? summary;

  /// `description`.
  final String? description;

  /// `deprecated`.
  final bool deprecated;

  /// The security requirements (R10.8): the operation's `security`, else
  /// the document's. Any one alternative is enough; every scheme of an
  /// alternative is needed; an empty alternative makes security optional.
  final List<List<SecurityUseIr>> security;

  /// The sealed type of the successes, when 2xx responses have different
  /// bodies (decision 18 of M8). Without it the success type is the one
  /// body type, `void` without a body, and `JsonValue` when no 2xx is
  /// documented (TOA653).
  final StatusTypeIr? successType;

  /// The sealed type of the errors, for [ErrorsByStatus].
  final StatusTypeIr? errorType;

  /// The class of the parts of a multipart body ([RequestBodyIr.parts]).
  final String? partsName;

  /// The key of the operation in the overrides file: its `operationId`,
  /// or `METHOD /path`.
  String get overrideKey => operationId ?? '$method $path';

  /// The responses with a 2xx status.
  List<ResponseIr> get successes => [
    for (final response in responses)
      if (response.isSuccess) response,
  ];
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/parameter_checks.dart`:

````dart
import '../model/operation.dart';
import 'ir_context.dart';
import 'parameter_styles.dart';

/// Checks a parameter before it is generated: whether it can be sent
/// (TOA565) and whether its style is allowed where it is (TOA564).
final class ParameterChecks {
  /// Creates the checks; they report through [_context].
  const ParameterChecks(this._context);

  final IrContext _context;

  /// Whether a parameter of [placement] can be sent; a `querystring`
  /// parameter cannot, and is reported (TOA565).
  bool isSendable(
    ParameterLocation placement,
    Parameter parameter,
    String wire,
  ) {
    if (placement != .querystring) return true;
    _context.report(
      .querystringNotGenerated,
      'Parameter "$wire" is in: querystring (OpenAPI 3.2), which the client '
      'cannot send; the parameter is not generated.',
      parameter.location,
      fix: 'Describe the query string as query parameters at the source.',
    );
    return false;
  }

  /// The style of [parameter] as the IR keeps it: what the spec writes when
  /// its placement allows it, else null (the default style of the
  /// placement), with TOA564.
  ParameterStyle? checkedStyle(
    Parameter parameter,
    ParameterLocation placement,
    String wire,
  ) {
    final style = parameter.style;
    final allowed = ParameterStyles.allowed[placement];
    if (style == null || allowed == null || allowed.contains(style)) {
      return style;
    }
    final fallback = allowed.first;
    _context.report(
      .parameterStyleInvalid,
      'The style "${style.name}" is not allowed for a ${placement.name} '
      'parameter, which takes '
      '${[for (final s in allowed) '"${s.name}"'].join(', ')}; the default '
      'style "${fallback.name}" is used for "$wire".',
      parameter.location,
      fix: 'Use one of these styles at the source.',
    );
    return null;
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/request_body_builder.dart`:

````dart
import '../model/operation.dart';
import '../model/schema.dart';
import 'body_encoding.dart';
import 'dart_type.dart';
import 'ir.dart';
import 'ir_context.dart';
import 'media_type_picker.dart';
import 'parts_builder.dart';
import 'schema_mapper.dart';

/// Builds the request body of an operation (R10.4): picks its media type and
/// maps its type, its parts (multipart) and its encoding (forms).
final class RequestBodyBuilder {
  /// Creates a builder that maps types with [_mapper].
  RequestBodyBuilder(this._context, this._mapper)
    : _parts = PartsBuilder(_context, _mapper),
      _encoding = BodyEncoding(_context),
      _picker = MediaTypePicker(_context);

  final IrContext _context;
  final SchemaMapper _mapper;
  final PartsBuilder _parts;
  final BodyEncoding _encoding;
  final MediaTypePicker _picker;

  /// The request body of [operation], sent with [method]; [prefix] starts
  /// the names of its inline types.
  RequestBodyIr? of(Operation operation, String method, String prefix) {
    final written = operation.requestBody;
    if (written == null) return null;
    final body = _context.model.resolve(written);
    if (body == null) return null;
    final (contentType, media) = _picker.pick(
      body.content,
      body.location,
      'request body',
    );
    final kind = contentType == null
        ? BodyKind.none
        : MediaTypePicker.kindOf(contentType);
    final schema = media?.schema;
    final parts = kind == .multipart ? _parts.of(media, prefix) : null;
    // Parts are a class of their own.
    final type = parts == null ? _bodyType(schema, kind, prefix, method) : null;
    return RequestBodyIr(
      kind: kind,
      location: body.location,
      contentType: contentType,
      type: type,
      required: body.required,
      description: body.description,
      encoding: _encoding.of(media, kind),
      parts: parts,
    );
  }

  /// The type of a request body of [kind]: raw bytes have no model, as in
  /// responses, and text is a string.
  DartType? _bodyType(
    Schema? schema,
    BodyKind kind,
    String prefix,
    String method,
  ) {
    return switch ((schema, kind)) {
      (_, .bytes) => const CoreType(.binary),
      (_, .text || .eventStream) => const CoreType(.string),
      (null, _) => null,
      (final schema?, _) => _mapper.typeOf(
        schema,
        NameHint(prefix, 'Request'),
        direction: .request,
        patchBody: method.toLowerCase() == 'patch',
      ),
    };
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/response_builder.dart`:

````dart
import '../model/operation.dart';
import '../model/schema.dart';
import 'dart_type.dart';
import 'ir.dart';
import 'ir_context.dart';
import 'media_type_picker.dart';
import 'response_secrets.dart';
import 'response_statuses.dart';
import 'schema_mapper.dart';

/// Builds the responses and the errors of an operation (R10.6, R11.4):
/// picks a media type per response, maps the body types and unwraps
/// envelopes (R6.4).
final class ResponseBuilder {
  /// Creates a builder that maps types with [_mapper].
  ResponseBuilder(this._context, this._mapper)
    : _secrets = ResponseSecrets(_context),
      _statuses = ResponseStatuses(_context),
      _picker = MediaTypePicker(_context);

  final IrContext _context;
  final SchemaMapper _mapper;
  final ResponseSecrets _secrets;
  final ResponseStatuses _statuses;
  final MediaTypePicker _picker;

  /// The generated responses of [operation] with the media type used for
  /// each, before any type is mapped.
  List<PickedResponse> pick(Operation operation) => [
    for (final (status, written) in _statuses.of(operation))
      if (_context.model.resolve(written) case final response?)
        (
          status,
          response,
          _picker.pick(response.content, response.location, 'response'),
        ),
  ];

  /// The IR of the picked [responses]; [prefix] starts the names of their
  /// inline types.
  List<ResponseIr> of(List<PickedResponse> responses, String prefix) {
    final withBody = ResponseStatuses.bodyCounts(responses);
    final result = <ResponseIr>[];
    for (final (status, response, (contentType, media)) in responses) {
      final kind = _responseKind(contentType, response);
      final success = status.startsWith('2');
      final base = ResponseStatuses.nameOf(status, shared: false);
      final suffix = ResponseStatuses.nameOf(
        status,
        shared: (withBody[base] ?? 0) > 1,
      );
      final schema = kind == .eventStream
          ? media?.itemSchema ?? media?.schema
          : media?.schema;
      final envelope = success && kind == .json && schema != null
          ? _envelope(schema)
          : null;
      final type = _bodyType(
        envelope ?? schema,
        kind,
        NameHint(prefix, suffix),
      );
      result.add(
        ResponseIr(
          status: status,
          kind: kind,
          location: response.location,
          contentType: contentType,
          type: type,
          envelopeKey: envelope == null ? null : 'data',
          description: response.description,
          sensitive: _secrets.isSecret(envelope ?? schema, type),
        ),
      );
    }
    return result;
  }

  /// The type of a response body of [kind] whose schema is [schema].
  DartType? _bodyType(Schema? schema, BodyKind kind, NameHint hint) =>
      switch ((schema, kind)) {
        (_, .text) => const CoreType(.string),
        (_, .bytes) => const CoreType(.binary),
        (final schema?, _) => _mapper.typeOf(
          schema,
          hint,
          direction: .response,
        ),
        _ => null,
      };

  /// How the body of [response] in [contentType] is read: a form or a
  /// multipart response is bytes (TOA650), since a client does not read
  /// forms.
  BodyKind _responseKind(String? contentType, Response response) {
    if (contentType == null) return .none;
    final kind = MediaTypePicker.kindOf(contentType);
    if (kind != .form && kind != .multipart) return kind;
    _context.report(
      .mediaTypeAsBytes,
      'The response is "$contentType", which the client reads as bytes; its '
      'schema is not used.',
      response.location.child('content').child(contentType),
      fix: 'Use a JSON media type at the source if the schema matters.',
    );
    return .bytes;
  }

  /// The schema of `data` when [schema] is an envelope `{data: T}`: an
  /// object whose only property is the required `data` (R6.4).
  Schema? _envelope(Schema schema) {
    final resolved = _context.model.resolveSchema(schema);
    if (resolved is! SchemaObject) return null;
    final data = resolved.properties['data'];
    if (data == null ||
        resolved.properties.length != 1 ||
        !resolved.required.contains('data') ||
        resolved.allOf.isNotEmpty ||
        resolved.additionalProperties is SchemaObject) {
      return null;
    }
    return data;
  }

  /// The documented errors (R11.4): `Never` without any, the one body
  /// type when all errors share it, else the body type of each status. A
  /// status without a body uses `errors.default_schema` when it is set.
  ErrorsIr errors(List<ResponseIr> responses) {
    final fallback = _defaultErrorType();
    final cases = [
      for (final response in responses)
        if (response.isError) (response.status, response.type ?? fallback),
    ];
    if (cases.isEmpty) {
      return fallback == null
          ? const NoErrors()
          : SingleError(fallback, const []);
    }
    final types = {for (final (_, type) in cases) type};
    if (types.length == 1 && types.single != null) {
      return SingleError(types.single!, [for (final (s, _) in cases) s]);
    }
    return ErrorsByStatus(
      cases,
      sensitive: _secrets.errorStatuses(responses, fallback),
    );
  }

  DartType? _defaultErrorType() {
    final key = _context.defaultErrorSchema;
    if (key == null) return null;
    final schema = _context.model.document.components?.schemas[key];
    if (schema == null) return null;
    return _mapper.typeOfComponent(schema, direction: .response);
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/spec_operation.dart`:

````dart
import '../model/operation.dart';

/// An operation of the spec with its method and path.
final class SpecOperation {
  /// Creates the entry for [operation], found at [method] of [path].
  const SpecOperation(this.operation, this.method, this.path, this.item);

  /// The Operation Object.
  final Operation operation;

  /// The HTTP method as the spec writes it: lower case for the fixed
  /// fields, as written for OpenAPI 3.2 `additionalOperations`.
  final String method;

  /// The path template.
  final String path;

  /// The Path Item Object the operation is in.
  final PathItem item;

  /// The key of the operation in the overrides file: its `operationId`, or
  /// `METHOD /path`.
  String get overrideKey =>
      operation.operationId ?? '${method.toUpperCase()} $path';
}
````

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: task 2: `ResponseBuilder`, `RequestBodyBuilder`, `SpecOperation`, `OperationIr`; `SchemaMapper.typeOf` (public API only); `NameScope.claim`; `IrContext.report`.
- Produces: `OperationIr.events` (`EventStreamIr`, `StreamReconnection`), `OperationBuilder.build(entry, name, methods)` returning a list, `MethodBuilder`, `EventStreams`, `StreamShape`, `EventData`, `GenerationConfig.streamSuffix` and `streamReconnect`, `DiagnosticCode.eventStreamMethodAdded` (TOA663), `eventDataArrayItems` (TOA664), `eventStreamReconnectOff` (TOA665), `eventItemSchemaUnread` (TOA666).

Spec decisions 1, 3, 5, 9; plan decisions 6-9. `OperationBuilder.build` keeps the parameters and hands the rest to `MethodBuilder`, which asks `EventStreams.shapeOf` and builds zero, one or two `OperationIr`s; `IrBuilder` passes the group's method scope so the stream name is claimed after every other method of the group. `ResponseBuilder.pick` picks the event-stream media type of each success for the stream method, and for the other method of an operation that has both does not report the event stream as dropped (TOA651). An event-stream response's type is now `String` (it was the mapped `itemSchema`, which declared an envelope model nobody used and did not match the `r.text` the decoder wrote). `ApiNames` gives a stream method no sealed success type. `IrJson` dumps `events`. The M9 tests that expected every event stream to be skipped (TOA654) now use the shapes M10 still cannot express. A non-GET stream does not reconnect, and only when the config's `sse.reconnect` is on is that reported (TOA665, once per operation, with the override that enables it as the fix); without a reconnect request there is no diagnostic. Hostile names: a method already named `chatStream` makes the stream method `chatStream2` with TOA551; recursion and secrets in event data go through the M8 mapper unchanged (tests). The `itemSchema` `oneOf` union by event name is task 5: until then it is text with TOA666.

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/config/operation_settings_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/config/config_loader.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/config/project_config.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diagnostics/diagnostic_bag.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diagnostics/diagnostic_code.dart';

import '../helpers/temp_dir.dart';

void main() {
  List<ClientConfig> load(String text, DiagnosticBag bag) =>
      ConfigLoader(bag).load(TempDir().write('c.yaml', text))!.clients;

  test('event streams: suffix Stream and no reconnection by default', () {
    final bag = DiagnosticBag();
    final config = load('''
clients:
  a: {input: a.json, output: lib/a}
''', bag).single.generation;
    expect(bag.all, isEmpty);
    expect(config.streamSuffix, 'Stream');
    expect(config.streamReconnect, isFalse);
  });

  test('a client overrides the stream settings of defaults', () {
    final bag = DiagnosticBag();
    final clients = load('''
defaults:
  naming: {stream_suffix: Events}
  sse: {reconnect: true}
clients:
  a: {input: a.json, output: lib/a}
  b:
    input: b.json
    output: lib/b
    naming: {stream_suffix: _live}
    sse: {reconnect: false}
''', bag);
    expect(bag.all, isEmpty);
    expect(clients[0].generation.streamSuffix, 'Events');
    expect(clients[0].generation.streamReconnect, isTrue);
    expect(clients[1].generation.streamSuffix, '_live');
    expect(clients[1].generation.streamReconnect, isFalse);
    expect(
      clients[0].generation.key,
      isNot(clients[1].generation.key),
    );
  });

  test('bad stream settings are TOA902 and the defaults are used', () {
    final bag = DiagnosticBag();
    final config = load('''
clients:
  a:
    input: a.json
    output: lib/a
    naming: {stream_suffix: 'my stream'}
    sse: {reconnect: 'yes', retry: 3}
''', bag).single.generation;
    expect(config.streamSuffix, 'Stream');
    expect(config.streamReconnect, isFalse);
    expect(
      [for (final d in bag.sorted) (d.code, d.pointer?.value)],
      [
        (DiagnosticCode.configInvalid, '#/clients/a/naming/stream_suffix'),
        (DiagnosticCode.configInvalid, '#/clients/a/sse/reconnect'),
        (DiagnosticCode.configUnknownKey, '#/clients/a/sse/retry'),
      ],
    );
  });
}
````

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/config/generation_config.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/ir/ir.dart';

import '../helpers/ir_fixture.dart';

/// A spec with [paths] (YAML under `paths:`) and [schemas] (under
/// `components.schemas`), in OpenAPI [version].
String _spec(String paths, {String schemas = '{}', String version = '3.1.0'}) =>
    '''
openapi: $version
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
paths:
$paths
components:
  schemas: $schemas
''';

/// The methods of the only group of [ir] by name.
Map<String, OperationIr> _methods(ClientIr ir) => {
  for (final o in ir.groups.single.operations) o.name: o,
};

/// The Dart type of the event data of [operation]: `String` for text.
String _data(ClientIr ir, OperationIr operation) =>
    operation.events!.data?.code(ir.nameOf) ?? 'String';

void main() {
  test('an event stream with a JSON schema is one method of its events', () {
    final fixture = IrFixture(
      _spec(
        r'''
  /feed:
    get:
      operationId: feed
      responses:
        "200": {description: ok, content: {text/event-stream: {schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Tick'}}}}
        "404": {description: no, content: {application/json: {schema: {type: string}}}}
''',
        schemas: '{Tick: {type: object, properties: {n: {type: integer}}}}',
      ),
    );
    final ir = fixture.build();
    final feed = _methods(ir)['feed']!;
    expect(_data(ir, feed), 'Tick');
    expect(feed.events!.reconnection, StreamReconnection.onRequest);
    expect(feed.successType, isNull);
    expect(
      [for (final r in feed.responses) r.kind],
      [
        BodyKind.eventStream,
        BodyKind.json,
      ],
    );
    expect(feed.errors, isA<SingleError>());
    expect(fixture.reportedOf(.eventStreamNotGenerated), isEmpty);
  });

  group('the data type', () {
    String dataOf(String media, {String schemas = '{}'}) {
      final fixture = IrFixture(
        _spec(
          '''
  /feed:
    get:
      operationId: feed
      responses:
        "200": {description: ok, content: {text/event-stream: $media}}
''',
          schemas: schemas,
          version: '3.2.0',
        ),
      );
      final ir = fixture.build();
      return _data(ir, _methods(ir)['feed']!);
    }

    test('is the contentSchema of itemSchema data, not the envelope', () {
      final fixture = IrFixture(
        _spec(
          r'''
  /feed:
    get:
      operationId: feed
      responses:
        "200":
          description: ok
          content:
            text/event-stream:
              itemSchema:
                type: object
                properties:
                  event: {type: string}
                  data: {type: string, contentMediaType: application/json, contentSchema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Tick'}}
''',
          schemas: '{Tick: {type: object, properties: {n: {type: integer}}}}',
          version: '3.2.0',
        ),
      );
      final ir = fixture.build();
      expect(_data(ir, _methods(ir)['feed']!), 'Tick');
      expect([for (final d in ir.declarations) d.name], ['Tick']);
    });

    test('is text for itemSchema data that is a string', () {
      expect(
        dataOf('{itemSchema: {properties: {data: {type: string}}}}'),
        'String',
      );
    });

    test('is text, reported, when itemSchema has no data (TOA666)', () {
      final fixture = IrFixture(
        _spec('''
  /feed:
    get:
      operationId: feed
      responses:
        "200": {description: ok, content: {text/event-stream: {itemSchema: {properties: {id: {type: string}}}}}}
''', version: '3.2.0'),
      );
      final ir = fixture.build();
      expect(_data(ir, _methods(ir)['feed']!), 'String');
      expect(
        fixture.reportedOf(.eventItemSchemaUnread).single.pointer?.value,
        '#/paths/~1feed/get/responses/200/content/text~1event-stream/'
        'itemSchema',
      );
    });

    test('is an item of an array schema, reported (TOA664)', () {
      final fixture = IrFixture(
        _spec(r'''
  /feed:
    get:
      operationId: feed
      responses:
        "200": {description: ok, content: {text/event-stream: {schema: {type: array, items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Tick'}}}}}
''', schemas: '{Tick: {type: object, properties: {n: {type: integer}}}}'),
      );
      final ir = fixture.build();
      expect(_data(ir, _methods(ir)['feed']!), 'Tick');
      expect(fixture.reportedOf(.eventDataArrayItems), hasLength(1));
    });

    test('is text for a string schema and for no schema', () {
      expect(dataOf('{schema: {type: string}}'), 'String');
      expect(dataOf('{}'), 'String');
    });

    test('is text for an itemSchema that cannot be resolved', () {
      expect(
        dataOf(r"{itemSchema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Gone'}}"),
        'String',
      );
    });

    test('may be recursive', () {
      expect(
        dataOf(
          r"{schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Node'}}",
          schemas:
              '{Node: {type: object, properties: {children: {type: array, '
              r"items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Node'}}}}}",
        ),
        'Node',
      );
    });
  });

  group('an operation that offers JSON and an event stream', () {
    const chat = r'''
  /chat:
    post:
      operationId: chat
      responses:
        "200":
          description: ok
          content:
            application/json: {schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Reply'}}
            text/event-stream: {schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Delta'}}
''';
    const schemas =
        '{Reply: {type: object, properties: {text: {type: string}}}, '
        'Delta: {type: object, properties: {part: {type: string}}}}';

    test('gets a second method for the events (TOA663)', () {
      final fixture = IrFixture(_spec(chat, schemas: schemas));
      final ir = fixture.build();
      final methods = _methods(ir);
      expect(methods.keys, ['chat', 'chatStream']);
      expect(methods['chat']!.events, isNull);
      expect(methods['chat']!.successes.single.kind, BodyKind.json);
      expect(_data(ir, methods['chatStream']!), 'Delta');
      expect(fixture.reportedOf(.eventStreamMethodAdded), hasLength(1));
      expect(fixture.reportedOf(.mediaTypesDropped), isEmpty);
    });

    test('names it with naming.stream_suffix', () {
      final fixture = IrFixture(
        _spec(chat, schemas: schemas),
        generation: const GenerationConfig(streamSuffix: 'Live'),
      );
      expect(_methods(fixture.build()).keys, ['chat', 'chatLive']);
    });

    test('names it apart from a method that has its name (TOA551)', () {
      final fixture = IrFixture(
        _spec(
          '''
$chat  /chat-stream:
    get:
      operationId: chatStream
      responses:
        "204": {description: none}
''',
          schemas: schemas,
        ),
      );
      expect(_methods(fixture.build()).keys, [
        'chat',
        'chatStream',
        'chatStream2',
      ]);
      expect(
        fixture.reportedOf(.nameCollision).single.message,
        contains('"chatStream2"'),
      );
    });

    test('of a POST does not reconnect, silently by default', () {
      final fixture = IrFixture(_spec(chat, schemas: schemas));
      final stream = _methods(fixture.build())['chatStream']!;
      expect(stream.events!.reconnection, StreamReconnection.off);
      expect(fixture.reportedOf(.eventStreamReconnectOff), isEmpty);
    });

    test(
      'of a POST is reported when the config asks to reconnect (TOA665)',
      () {
        final fixture = IrFixture(
          _spec(chat, schemas: schemas),
          generation: const GenerationConfig(streamReconnect: true),
        );
        final stream = _methods(fixture.build())['chatStream']!;
        expect(stream.events!.reconnection, StreamReconnection.off);
        final reported = fixture.reportedOf(.eventStreamReconnectOff).single;
        expect(reported.pointer?.value, '#/paths/~1chat/post');
        expect(reported.message, contains('applies to GET event streams only'));
        expect(reported.fix, contains('operations:'));
        expect(reported.fix, contains('reconnect: true'));
      },
    );
  });

  test('a GET reconnects by default with sse.reconnect in the config', () {
    final fixture = IrFixture(
      _spec('''
  /feed:
    get:
      operationId: feed
      responses:
        "200": {description: ok, content: {text/event-stream: {}}}
        "204": {description: no events}
'''),
      generation: const GenerationConfig(streamReconnect: true),
    );
    final feed = _methods(fixture.build())['feed']!;
    expect(feed.events!.reconnection, StreamReconnection.on);
    expect([for (final r in feed.successes) r.status], ['200', '204']);
    expect(fixture.reportedOf(.eventStreamReconnectOff), isEmpty);
  });

  test('event streams with different data are not generated (TOA654)', () {
    final fixture = IrFixture(
      _spec('''
  /feed:
    get:
      operationId: feed
      responses:
        "200": {description: ok, content: {text/event-stream: {schema: {type: object, properties: {a: {type: string}}}}}}
        "206": {description: part, content: {text/event-stream: {schema: {type: string}}}}
'''),
    );
    final ir = fixture.build();
    expect(ir.groups, isEmpty);
    expect(
      fixture.reportedOf(.eventStreamNotGenerated).single.pointer?.value,
      '#/paths/~1feed/get/responses',
    );
  });

  test('a secret in event data is a secret field of its model', () {
    final fixture = IrFixture(
      _spec(
        r'''
  /feed:
    get:
      operationId: feed
      responses:
        "200": {description: ok, content: {text/event-stream: {schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Token'}}}}
''',
        schemas:
            '{Token: {type: object, properties: {value: {type: string, '
            'format: password}}}}',
      ),
    );
    final model = fixture.build().declarations.single as ModelDecl;
    expect(model.fields.single.sensitive, isTrue);
  });
}
````
