- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/config/operation_settings_reader.dart`:

````dart
import '../diagnostics/json_pointer.dart';
import 'generation_config.dart';

/// Reads the settings of how a client's operations are generated:
/// `naming.operation_prefix`, `naming.stream_suffix` and `sse.reconnect`,
/// each from the last source that has it (`defaults` first). A bad value
/// is reported through the `invalid` function (TOA902) and the default is
/// used.
final class OperationSettingsReader {
  /// Creates a reader of [_sources] that reports through [_invalid].
  const OperationSettingsReader(this._sources, this._invalid);

  /// The maps to read, `defaults` first, with their pointers.
  final List<(Map<String, Object?>, JsonPointer)> _sources;

  final void Function(String message, JsonPointer at) _invalid;

  static final _identifierPart = RegExp(r'^[A-Za-z0-9_$]+$');

  /// `naming.operation_prefix`: what is removed from the start of an
  /// `operationId`; null when not set or not a valid regular expression.
  RegExp? operationPrefix() {
    final picked = _pick('naming', 'operation_prefix');
    if (picked == null) return null;
    final (value, at) = picked;
    if (value is! String || value.isEmpty) {
      _invalid('"operation_prefix" must be a non-empty string.', at);
      return null;
    }
    try {
      return RegExp(value);
    } on FormatException catch (e) {
      _invalid(
        '"operation_prefix" is not a valid regular expression: '
        '${e.message}.',
        at,
      );
      return null;
    }
  }

  /// `naming.stream_suffix`: what the name of the event-stream method of an
  /// operation that also returns JSON ends with.
  String streamSuffix() {
    final picked = _pick('naming', 'stream_suffix');
    if (picked == null) return GenerationConfig.defaultStreamSuffix;
    final (value, at) = picked;
    if (value is String && _identifierPart.hasMatch(value)) return value;
    _invalid(
      r'"stream_suffix" must be letters, digits, "_" or "$", such as '
      'Stream; ${GenerationConfig.defaultStreamSuffix} is used.',
      at,
    );
    return GenerationConfig.defaultStreamSuffix;
  }

  /// `sse.reconnect`: whether the event streams of GET operations
  /// reconnect unless a call says no.
  bool streamReconnect() {
    final picked = _pick('sse', 'reconnect');
    if (picked == null) return false;
    final (value, at) = picked;
    if (value is bool) return value;
    _invalid('"reconnect" must be true or false.', at);
    return false;
  }

  (Object?, JsonPointer)? _pick(String section, String key) {
    for (final (map, at) in _sources.reversed) {
      final inner = map[section];
      if (inner is Map<String, Object?> && inner.containsKey(key)) {
        return (inner[key], at.child(section).child(key));
      }
    }
    return null;
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/event_data.dart`:

````dart
import '../model/media_type.dart';
import '../model/media_type_name.dart';
import '../model/schema.dart';
import '../model/spec_node.dart';
import 'dart_type.dart';
import 'ir_context.dart';
import 'schema_mapper.dart';

/// The type of the data of each event of a `text/event-stream` media type
/// (decision 1 of M10): `itemSchema` (OpenAPI 3.2) describes the whole
/// event and its `data` property the data; `schema`, the code-first
/// practice, describes the JSON in `data`, an array its items.
final class EventData {
  /// Creates the reader; it maps types with [_mapper].
  const EventData(this._context, this._mapper);

  final IrContext _context;
  final SchemaMapper _mapper;

  /// The type of each event's data in [media] at [location]; null when the
  /// data is text. Inline schemas are named after [hint].
  DartType? of(MediaType? media, SpecLocation location, NameHint hint) {
    if (media == null) return null;
    if (media.itemSchema case final item?) {
      return _fromItem(item, location.child('itemSchema'), hint);
    }
    final schema = media.schema;
    if (schema == null) return null;
    final resolved = _context.model.resolveSchema(schema);
    if (resolved is SchemaObject &&
        resolved.types.contains(SchemaType.array) &&
        resolved.items != null) {
      _context.report(
        .eventDataArrayItems,
        'The event stream\'s schema is an array; the data of each event is '
        'taken to be one of its items.',
        location.child('schema'),
        fix: 'Describe one event at the source (itemSchema in OpenAPI 3.2).',
      );
      return _json(resolved.items!, hint);
    }
    return _json(schema, hint);
  }

  /// The data type an `itemSchema` gives: JSON in a `contentSchema`, else
  /// text, reported when `data` says neither.
  DartType? _fromItem(Schema item, SpecLocation location, NameHint hint) {
    final resolved = _context.model.resolveSchema(item);
    final data = resolved is SchemaObject ? resolved.properties['data'] : null;
    final schema = data == null ? null : _context.model.resolveSchema(data);
    if (schema is SchemaObject) {
      final contentSchema = schema.contentSchema;
      final mediaType = schema.contentMediaType;
      if (contentSchema != null &&
          mediaType != null &&
          MediaTypeName(mediaType).isJson) {
        return _json(contentSchema, hint);
      }
      if (contentSchema == null &&
          mediaType == null &&
          (schema.types.isEmpty || schema.types.contains(SchemaType.string))) {
        return null;
      }
    }
    _context.report(
      .eventItemSchemaUnread,
      'The itemSchema does not say what the data of an event is: it needs a '
      '"data" property that is a string, or JSON with a "contentSchema"; the '
      'data is read as text.',
      location,
      fix:
          'Give "data" contentMediaType: application/json and a '
          'contentSchema at the source.',
    );
    return null;
  }

  /// The type of JSON data of [schema]; null when it is a plain string,
  /// which an event carries as text, not as a JSON string.
  DartType? _json(Schema schema, NameHint hint) {
    final type = _mapper.typeOf(schema, hint, direction: .response);
    return type == const CoreType(.string) ? null : type;
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/event_stream_ir.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart_type.dart';

/// Whether the stream of an event-stream method reconnects (R12.5), as the
/// runtime's `SseReconnection` says: a GET reconnects when the call asks
/// for it, an operation that enables reconnection (config or override)
/// unless the call says no, any other method never.
enum StreamReconnection {
  /// Never; a call cannot turn it on.
  off,

  /// When the call asks for it.
  onRequest,

  /// Unless the call says no.
  on,
}

/// The events of an event-stream method (R12.1): what the data of each
/// event decodes to and how the stream reconnects.
final class EventStreamIr {
  /// Creates the events of a method.
  const EventStreamIr({this.data, this.reconnection = .onRequest});

  /// The type the JSON data of each event decodes to; null when the data
  /// is text, a `String`.
  final DartType? data;

  /// How the stream reconnects.
  final StreamReconnection reconnection;
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/event_streams.dart`:

````dart
import '../model/operation.dart';
import 'event_data.dart';
import 'ir.dart';
import 'ir_context.dart';
import 'media_type_picker.dart';
import 'names.dart';
import 'operation_builder.dart';
import 'response_statuses.dart';
import 'schema_mapper.dart';

/// How the successes of an operation use `text/event-stream`.
enum StreamShape {
  /// No success is an event stream.
  none,

  /// Every success with a body is only an event stream: one method that
  /// returns the events.
  only,

  /// Every success with an event stream offers another body too: the
  /// operation's method and a second one that returns the events.
  both,

  /// Some successes are event streams and others have a body that is not
  /// (TOA654): not generated.
  mixed,
}

/// Decides how an operation's event streams are generated (decisions 1, 3
/// and 5 of M10): its [StreamShape], the name of a second method, the type
/// of the events and how the stream reconnects.
final class EventStreams {
  /// Creates the decisions; types are mapped with [mapper].
  EventStreams(this._context, SchemaMapper mapper)
    : _statuses = ResponseStatuses(_context),
      _data = EventData(_context, mapper);

  final IrContext _context;
  final ResponseStatuses _statuses;
  final EventData _data;

  /// How the successes of [operation] use event streams; [StreamShape.mixed]
  /// is reported (TOA654).
  StreamShape shapeOf(Operation operation) {
    var streams = 0;
    var alone = 0;
    var others = 0;
    for (final (status, written) in _statuses.of(operation)) {
      final response = _context.model.resolve(written);
      if (!status.startsWith('2') || response == null) continue;
      final kinds = {
        for (final type in response.content.keys) MediaTypePicker.kindOf(type),
      };
      if (!kinds.contains(BodyKind.eventStream)) {
        if (kinds.isNotEmpty) others++;
        continue;
      }
      streams++;
      if (kinds.length == 1) alone++;
    }
    if (streams == 0) return .none;
    if (others == 0 && (alone == streams || alone == 0)) {
      return alone == streams ? .only : .both;
    }
    _context.report(
      .eventStreamNotGenerated,
      'Some successes of this operation are event streams and others have '
      'a body that is not, so neither a method that returns the body nor '
      'one that returns the events covers them; the operation is not '
      'generated.',
      operation.location.child('responses'),
      fix: 'Offer text/event-stream on every success with a body.',
    );
    return .mixed;
  }

  /// The name of the second method of an operation named [name] that also
  /// returns JSON: [name] with `naming.stream_suffix`, claimed in the group's
  /// [methods] after every other method (TOA663, and TOA551 when taken).
  String methodName(SpecOperation entry, String name, NameScope methods) {
    final candidate = '$name${_context.config.streamSuffix}';
    final (claimed, change) = methods.claim(candidate);
    final location = entry.operation.location;
    _context.report(
      .eventStreamMethodAdded,
      'This operation returns JSON or an event stream; "$name" returns the '
      'JSON and "$claimed" the events.',
      location,
      fix: 'Set naming.stream_suffix in the config to name it otherwise.',
    );
    if (change != null) {
      _context.report(
        change == .reserved ? .nameReserved : .nameCollision,
        'Another method of the group is named "$candidate"; the event-stream '
        'method of ${entry.overrideKey} is named "$claimed".',
        location,
        fix: 'Set naming.stream_suffix in the config, or rename the method.',
      );
    }
    return claimed;
  }

  /// The events of the stream successes of [picked] (their media types are
  /// `text/event-stream`), named after [prefix]; null, reported (TOA654),
  /// when they disagree on the type of an event.
  EventStreamIr? events(
    SpecOperation entry,
    List<PickedResponse> picked,
    String prefix,
  ) {
    final types = {
      for (final (status, response, (contentType, media)) in picked)
        if (status.startsWith('2') && contentType != null)
          _data.of(
            media,
            response.location.child('content').child(contentType),
            NameHint(prefix, 'Event'),
          ),
    };
    if (types.length > 1) {
      _context.report(
        .eventStreamNotGenerated,
        'The event-stream successes of this operation have different event '
        'types, so one stream cannot hold them; the operation is not '
        'generated.',
        entry.operation.location.child('responses'),
        fix: 'Give every event-stream success the same schema at the source.',
      );
      return null;
    }
    return EventStreamIr(
      data: types.firstOrNull,
      reconnection: _reconnection(entry),
    );
  }

  /// How the stream of [entry] reconnects: a GET when the call asks, or
  /// unless it says no with `sse.reconnect` in the config; any other method
  /// never, reported (TOA665) only when the config asks for reconnection,
  /// since it does not apply there.
  StreamReconnection _reconnection(SpecOperation entry) {
    final config = _context.config.streamReconnect;
    if (entry.method == 'get') return config ? .on : .onRequest;
    if (config) {
      _context.report(
        .eventStreamReconnectOff,
        'sse.reconnect in the config applies to GET event streams only, so '
        'this ${entry.method.toUpperCase()} operation does not reconnect: '
        'repeating the request may not be safe.',
        entry.operation.location,
        fix: 'Enable it for this operation if the request can be repeated.',
        override: _context.overrideAt(
          ['operations', entry.overrideKey],
          {
            'sse': {'reconnect': true},
          },
        ),
      );
    }
    return .off;
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/method_builder.dart`:

````dart
import 'event_streams.dart';
import 'ir.dart';
import 'ir_context.dart';
import 'media_type_picker.dart';
import 'names.dart';
import 'operation_checks.dart';
import 'request_body_builder.dart';
import 'response_builder.dart';
import 'schema_mapper.dart';
import 'security_builder.dart';
import 'spec_operation.dart';

/// Builds the methods of one operation: the method that returns its body
/// and, when it offers an event stream, the one that returns the events
/// (decision 5 of M10), with their responses, body, errors and security.
final class MethodBuilder {
  /// Creates a builder that maps types with [mapper].
  MethodBuilder(IrContext context, SchemaMapper mapper)
    : _checks = OperationChecks(context),
      _security = SecurityBuilder(context),
      _bodies = RequestBodyBuilder(context, mapper),
      _responses = ResponseBuilder(context, mapper),
      _streams = EventStreams(context, mapper);

  static const _names = DartNames();

  final OperationChecks _checks;
  final SecurityBuilder _security;
  final RequestBodyBuilder _bodies;
  final ResponseBuilder _responses;
  final EventStreams _streams;

  /// The methods of [entry] with [parameters]: the one named [name], and
  /// for an operation that returns JSON or an event stream also one that
  /// returns the events, named in the group's [methods] (TOA663). Empty when
  /// its event streams cannot be expressed (TOA654).
  List<OperationIr> of(
    SpecOperation entry,
    String name,
    List<ParameterIr> parameters,
    NameScope methods,
  ) {
    final shape = _streams.shapeOf(entry.operation);
    final main = switch (shape) {
      .none || .both => _operation(
        entry,
        name,
        parameters,
        _responses.pick(entry.operation, hasStreamMethod: shape == .both),
      ),
      .only || .mixed => null,
    };
    final stream = switch (shape) {
      .only => _streamOperation(entry, name, parameters),
      .both => _streamOperation(
        entry,
        _streams.methodName(entry, name, methods),
        parameters,
      ),
      .none || .mixed => null,
    };
    return [?main, ?stream];
  }

  /// The method of [entry] that returns its events, or null (TOA654).
  OperationIr? _streamOperation(
    SpecOperation entry,
    String name,
    List<ParameterIr> parameters,
  ) {
    final picked = _responses.pick(entry.operation, events: true);
    final events = _streams.events(
      entry,
      picked,
      _names.pascal(name) ?? name,
    );
    if (events == null) return null;
    return _operation(entry, name, parameters, picked, events: events);
  }

  OperationIr _operation(
    SpecOperation entry,
    String name,
    List<ParameterIr> parameters,
    List<PickedResponse> picked, {
    EventStreamIr? events,
  }) {
    final operation = entry.operation;
    final prefix = _names.pascal(name) ?? name;
    final responses = _responses.of(picked, prefix);
    _checks.successes(operation);
    _checks.servers(entry);
    _checks.callbacks(operation);
    return OperationIr(
      location: operation.location,
      name: name,
      method: entry.method.toUpperCase(),
      path: entry.path,
      operationId: operation.operationId,
      tags: operation.tags,
      summary: operation.summary,
      description: operation.description,
      deprecated: operation.deprecated,
      parameters: parameters,
      body: _bodies.of(operation, entry.method, prefix),
      responses: responses,
      errors: _responses.errors(responses),
      security: _security.of(operation),
      events: events,
    );
  }
}
````

### Task 4: Emitter: event-stream methods, goldens, the analyzer gate and real calls

**Files:**
- Modify: `CLAUDE.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/README.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/README.ua.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/decoder_writer.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/event_stream_writer.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/operation_emitter.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/event_stream_emit_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/fixtures/chat.yaml`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/generated_client_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/programs/stream_calls.dart.txt`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/test/dio/web_test.dart`
- Create (by `UPDATE_GOLDENS=1`): `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/goldens/chat/`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: task 1: `openEvents(..., reconnection:)`, `SseOptions`, `SseReconnecting`; task 3: `OperationIr.events`, `EventStreamIr`, `StreamReconnection`; M9: `OperationEmitter`, `DecoderWriter`, `RequestWriter`, `emitClient`.
- Produces: `EventStreamWriter`; `DecoderWriter.decode` (was `_decode`); the chat fixture and its goldens; `bin/stream_calls.dart` in the analyzer gate's package.

Spec decisions 2, 7, 8. A method with `events` calls `_sender.openEvents` instead of `send`: `streamStatuses` are its 2xx statuses (a range as every code in it), the event decoder is `(m) => ...` over `m.json` (`m.data` for text), `reconnection:` is written unless it is `onRequest`, and its doc comment says how it reconnects: for `off` (a non-GET the override does not enable) the one sentence "It does not reconnect unless the operation's `sse.reconnect` override enables it." `DecoderWriter` makes the success type `Stream<SseItem<T>>`, keeps only the error arms (the stream opens for its statuses before the decoder is asked) and reads error bodies with `r.json` (`openEvents` parses them). The chat fixture (OpenAPI 3.2) has a JSON+SSE POST (`chat`, `chatStream2`, since `chatStream` is taken), a GET whose `itemSchema.data` has a `contentSchema` with recursion (`Thread.replies`) and a 204, a text stream and a secret in event data (`Delta.sessionToken`, `format: password`). The real-calls program covers events, a decode error mid-stream, a disconnect, a documented error before the stream opens, a POST that ignores `reconnect: true`, reconnect with `Last-Event-ID` and the server's `retry` (and the reset of the attempts after an event), the backoff cap and the attempt limit, cancel during a wait and during the stream, text events and an undocumented status. The redacted `toString` of `Delta` shows that a secret in event data is never printed. The runtime's browser test gets a reconnecting stream on Dio's default adapter (spec decision 8): `TransportError`, and no wait.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/event_stream_emit_test.dart`:

````dart
@TestOn('vm')
library;

import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:test/test.dart';

import '../helpers/emit_fixture.dart';

void main() {
  final files = emitClient('chat.yaml');
  final chat = files['api/chat_api.dart']!;
  final threads = files['api/threads_api.dart']!;

  test('the chat fixture matches its goldens (R12.1)', () {
    final goldens = p.join('test', 'emit', 'goldens', 'chat');
    if (Platform.environment['UPDATE_GOLDENS'] == '1') {
      for (final MapEntry(:key, :value) in files.entries) {
        File(p.join(goldens, '$key.golden'))
          ..createSync(recursive: true)
          ..writeAsStringSync(value);
      }
    }
    final expected = {
      for (final file in Directory(
        goldens,
      ).listSync(recursive: true).whereType<File>())
        p
            .relative(file.path, from: goldens)
            .replaceAll(r'\', '/')
            .replaceAll(RegExp(r'\.golden$'), ''): file
            .readAsStringSync(),
    };
    expect(files.keys.toSet(), expected.keys.toSet());
    for (final MapEntry(:key, :value) in files.entries) {
      expect(value, expected[key], reason: key);
    }
  });

  test('JSON and an event stream give two methods', () {
    expect(chat, contains('Future<Result<Reply, Problem>> chat({'));
    expect(
      chat,
      contains(
        'Future<Result<Stream<SseItem<Delta>>, Problem>> chatStream2({',
      ),
    );
    expect(chat, contains('}) => _sender.openEvents('));
  });

  test('a stream method opens for its 2xx statuses only', () {
    expect(threads, contains('streamStatuses: {200, 204},'));
    expect(threads, contains('(m) => Thread.fromJson(JsonReader.asObject('));
  });

  test('a POST stream does not reconnect, a GET does when asked', () {
    expect(chat, contains('reconnection: .off,'));
    expect(
      chat,
      contains(
        "/// with `SseDisconnected`. It does not reconnect unless the operation's"
        '\n  /// `sse.reconnect` override enables it.',
      ),
    );
    expect(threads, isNot(contains('reconnection:')));
  });

  test('text events are the data as it is', () {
    expect(threads, contains('Stream<SseItem<String>>'));
    expect(threads, contains('(m) => m.data,'));
  });
}
````

````yaml
openapi: 3.2.0
info: {title: Chat, version: "1"}
paths:
  /chat:
    post:
      operationId: chat
      tags: [chat]
      summary: Answers a prompt, whole or as it is written.
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json: {schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Prompt'}}
      responses:
        "200":
          description: The answer.
          content:
            application/json: {schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Reply'}}
            text/event-stream: {schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Delta'}}
        "400":
          description: A bad prompt.
          content:
            application/json: {schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Problem'}}
  /chat/stream:
    get:
      operationId: chatStream
      tags: [chat]
      summary: Taken by an older method, so the stream of chat is chatStream2.
      responses:
        "204": {description: Nothing.}
  /threads/{threadId}/events:
    get:
      operationId: watchThread
      tags: [threads]
      parameters:
        - {name: threadId, in: path, required: true, schema: {type: string}}
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Changes to the thread, one event each.
          content:
            text/event-stream:
              itemSchema:
                type: object
                properties:
                  event: {type: string}
                  data:
                    type: string
                    contentMediaType: application/json
                    contentSchema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Thread'}
        "204": {description: The thread is closed.}
        "404":
          description: No such thread.
          content:
            application/json: {schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Problem'}}
  /log:
    get:
      operationId: tailLog
      tags: [threads]
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Log lines as text.
          content:
            text/event-stream: {schema: {type: string}}
components:
  schemas:
    Prompt:
      type: object
      required: [text]
      properties:
        text: {type: string}
    Reply:
      type: object
      required: [text]
      properties:
        text: {type: string}
    Delta:
      type: object
      required: [part]
      properties:
        part: {type: string}
        sessionToken: {type: string, format: password}
    Thread:
      type: object
      required: [id]
      properties:
        id: {type: string}
        replies: {type: array, items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Thread'}}
    Problem:
      type: object
      required: [code]
      properties:
        code: {type: string}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/programs/stream_calls.dart.txt`:

````dart
// A program that calls the event-stream methods of the client generated
// from test/emit/fixtures/chat.yaml through a Dio whose adapter answers from
// memory, and prints what was sent and every item each stream gave.
// The test reads what the program prints, so it prints.
// ignore_for_file: avoid_print
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:fixture/chat_raw/api_client.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_runtime/typed_openapi_runtime.dart';

/// Answers each request with the next of [answers]; the last one repeats.
final class Adapter implements HttpClientAdapter {
  Adapter(this.answers);

  final List<ResponseBody Function(RequestOptions)> answers;
  final List<RequestOptions> requests = [];
  int _next = 0;

  @override
  Future<ResponseBody> fetch(
    RequestOptions options,
    Stream<Uint8List>? requestStream,
    Future<void>? cancelFuture,
  ) async {
    requests.add(options);
    await requestStream?.drain<void>();
    final answer = answers[_next];
    if (_next < answers.length - 1) _next++;
    return answer(options);
  }

  @override
  void close({bool force = false}) {}
}

ResponseBody events(String text, [int status = 200]) => ResponseBody(
  Stream.value(Uint8List.fromList(utf8.encode(text))),
  status,
  headers: {
    Headers.contentTypeHeader: ['text/event-stream'],
  },
);

ResponseBody json(int status, Object? body) => ResponseBody.fromString(
  jsonEncode(body),
  status,
  headers: {
    Headers.contentTypeHeader: ['application/json'],
  },
);

ResponseBody refused(RequestOptions options) => throw DioException(
  requestOptions: options,
  type: DioExceptionType.connectionError,
  message: 'refused',
);

String describe<T>(SseItem<T> item) => switch (item) {
  SseEvent(:final data, :final event, :final id) => 'event $event #$id $data',
  SseDecodeError(:final error) => 'decode error ${error.pointer}',
  SseReconnecting(:final attempt, :final delay) =>
    'reconnecting $attempt after ${delay.inMilliseconds}ms',
  SseDisconnected(cause: DioException(:final type)) => 'end ${type.name}',
  SseDisconnected(cause: HttpError(:final statusCode)) => 'end http $statusCode',
  SseDisconnected(:final cause) => 'end ${cause ?? 'closed'}',
};

Future<void> printAll<T, E>(Result<Stream<SseItem<T>>, E> result) async {
  switch (result) {
    case Success(:final data, :final statusCode):
      print('success $statusCode');
      await for (final item in data) {
        print('  ${describe(item)}');
      }
    case HttpError(:final statusCode, :final body):
      print('error $statusCode $body');
    case Failure():
      print('failure $result');
  }
}

ApiClient client(Adapter adapter) =>
    ApiClient(Dio(BaseOptions(baseUrl: 'https://chat.example.com'))
      ..httpClientAdapter = adapter);

Future<void> main() async {
  // Events of a JSON type, a decode error mid-stream, a disconnect.
  var adapter = Adapter([
    (_) => events(
      'data: {"part": "Hel", "sessionToken": "s1"}\n\n'
      'data: {"nope": 1}\n\n'
      'event: delta\nid: 2\ndata: {"part": "lo"}\n\n',
    ),
  ]);
  await printAll(
    await client(adapter).chat.chatStream2(body: const Prompt(text: 'hi')),
  );
  final sent = adapter.requests.single;
  print('${sent.method} ${sent.uri} accept ${sent.headers['Accept']}');

  // A documented error before the stream opens.
  adapter = Adapter([(_) => json(400, {'code': 'empty'})]);
  await printAll(
    await client(adapter).chat.chatStream2(body: const Prompt(text: '')),
  );

  // A POST does not reconnect even when the call asks.
  adapter = Adapter([(_) => events('data: {"part": "a"}\n\n')]);
  await printAll(
    await client(adapter).chat.chatStream2(
      body: const Prompt(text: 'a'),
      call: const CallOptions(sse: SseOptions(reconnect: true)),
    ),
  );
  print('requests ${adapter.requests.length}');

  // A GET reconnects with Last-Event-ID and the server's retry, then the
  // thread closes with 204.
  final waits = <int>[];
  adapter = Adapter([
    (_) => events('retry: 1500\nid: 7\ndata: {"id": "t1", "replies": []}\n\n'),
    (_) => events('data: {"id": "t2"}\n\n'),
    (_) => events('', 204),
  ]);
  await printAll(
    await client(adapter).threads.watchThread(
      threadId: 'a/b',
      call: CallOptions(
        sse: SseOptions(
          reconnect: true,
          wait: (delay) async => waits.add(delay.inMilliseconds),
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
  print('waits $waits');
  print('last event ids ${[
    for (final r in adapter.requests) r.headers['Last-Event-ID'],
  ]}');
  print('path ${adapter.requests.first.uri.path}');

  // Failed attempts back off up to the cap and stop at the limit.
  waits.clear();
  adapter = Adapter([(_) => events('data: {"id": "t1"}\n\n'), refused]);
  await printAll(
    await client(adapter).threads.watchThread(
      threadId: 't',
      call: CallOptions(
        sse: SseOptions(
          reconnect: true,
          maxAttempts: 3,
          maxDelay: const Duration(milliseconds: 1500),
          wait: (delay) async => waits.add(delay.inMilliseconds),
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
  print('waits $waits');

  // Cancelling during the wait before an attempt ends the stream.
  final token = CancelToken();
  adapter = Adapter([(_) => events('data: {"id": "t1"}\n\n')]);
  final waiting = await client(adapter).threads.watchThread(
    threadId: 't',
    call: CallOptions(
      cancelToken: token,
      sse: SseOptions(reconnect: true, wait: (_) => Completer<void>().future),
    ),
  );
  if (waiting case Success(:final data)) {
    await for (final item in data) {
      print('  ${describe(item)}');
      if (item is SseReconnecting) token.cancel();
    }
  }
  print('requests ${adapter.requests.length}');

  // Cancelling while events arrive ends the stream without a reconnect.
  final live = CancelToken();
  final body = StreamController<Uint8List>();
  adapter = Adapter([
    (_) => ResponseBody(
      body.stream,
      200,
      headers: {
        Headers.contentTypeHeader: ['text/event-stream'],
      },
    ),
  ]);
  final open = await client(adapter).threads.watchThread(
    threadId: 't',
    call: CallOptions(
      cancelToken: live,
      sse: const SseOptions(reconnect: true),
    ),
  );
  body.add(Uint8List.fromList(utf8.encode('data: {"id": "t1"}\n\n')));
  if (open case Success(:final data)) {
    await for (final item in data) {
      print('  ${describe(item)}');
      if (item is SseEvent) live.cancel();
    }
  }
  await body.close();

  // Text events and an undocumented status.
  adapter = Adapter([(_) => events('data: line 1\ndata: line 2\n\n')]);
  await printAll(await client(adapter).threads.tailLog());
  adapter = Adapter([(_) => json(503, {'down': true})]);
  await printAll(await client(adapter).threads.tailLog());
}
````

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the tests to see them fail**

Run from `packages/typed_openapi`: `dart test test/emit/event_stream_emit_test.dart`.
Expected: FAIL: `chatStream2` is generated with `_sender.send(` and a `Result<String, Problem>` (the emitter does not know `events` yet), and the goldens directory `test/emit/goldens/chat` does not exist.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/event_stream_writer.dart`:

````dart
import '../ir/ir.dart';
import 'dart_writer.dart';
import 'decoder_writer.dart';

/// Writes what a method that returns events adds to the M9 shape (R12.1,
/// R12.5): the statuses that open the stream, the decoder of one event's
/// data, how the stream reconnects and a doc paragraph about it.
final class EventStreamWriter {
  /// Creates a writer that decodes JSON with [_decoder].
  const EventStreamWriter(this._decoder);

  final DecoderWriter _decoder;

  /// Writes the `streamStatuses` of the request of [operation]: its 2xx
  /// statuses, a range as every code in it.
  void statuses(DartWriter out, OperationIr operation) {
    final codes = [
      for (final response in operation.successes)
        if (int.tryParse(response.status) case final code?) '$code',
    ];
    final ranges = [
      for (final response in operation.successes)
        if (int.tryParse(response.status) == null) response.status,
    ];
    final elements = [
      ...codes,
      if (ranges.isNotEmpty) 'for (var s = 200; s < 300; s++) s',
    ];
    out.line('streamStatuses: {${elements.join(', ')}},');
  }

  /// The function that decodes the data of one event of [events].
  String decoder(EventStreamIr events) => switch (events.data) {
    null => '(m) => m.data',
    final data => '(m) => ${_decoder.decode(data, 'm.json')}',
  };

  /// Writes the named arguments after `call` of `openEvents`.
  void reconnection(DartWriter out, EventStreamIr events) {
    if (events.reconnection == .onRequest) return;
    out.line('reconnection: .${events.reconnection.name},');
  }

  /// The doc paragraph of a method that returns [events].
  String doc(EventStreamIr events) {
    final reconnect = switch (events.reconnection) {
      .off =>
        "It does not reconnect unless the operation's `sse.reconnect` "
            'override enables it.',
      .onRequest =>
        'It reconnects with `Last-Event-ID` when `call.sse` asks for it.',
      .on => 'It reconnects with `Last-Event-ID` unless `call.sse` says no.',
    };
    return 'Returns the events of `text/event-stream` as they arrive; the '
        'stream ends with `SseDisconnected`. $reconnect';
  }
}
````

````dart
// GENERATED CODE - DO NOT MODIFY BY HAND.
// dart format off

import 'package:dio/dio.dart' show Dio;
import 'package:typed_openapi_runtime/typed_openapi_runtime.dart'
    show
        CallOptions,
        DioRequestSender,
        JsonReader,
        OperationInfo,
        OperationRequest,
        RawResponse,
        Result,
        SseItem;

import '../models/delta.dart';
import '../models/problem.dart';
import '../models/prompt.dart';
import '../models/reply.dart';

/// The operations of the `ChatApi` group.
class ChatApi {
  /// Sends the requests through [dio], which it uses as it is.
  ChatApi(Dio dio) : _sender = DioRequestSender(dio);

  final DioRequestSender _sender;

  static const _chat = OperationInfo(
    operationId: 'chat',
    method: 'POST',
    pathTemplate: '/chat',
    tags: ['chat'],
  );

  static const _chatStream = OperationInfo(
    operationId: 'chatStream',
    method: 'GET',
    pathTemplate: '/chat/stream',
    tags: ['chat'],
  );

  static const _chatStream2 = OperationInfo(
    operationId: 'chat',
    method: 'POST',
    pathTemplate: '/chat',
    tags: ['chat'],
  );

  /// Answers a prompt, whole or as it is written.
  ///
  /// `POST /chat` (`chat`).
  Future<Result<Reply, Problem>> chat({
    required Prompt body,
    CallOptions? call,
  }) => _sender.send(
    OperationRequest(
      _chat,
      body: body.toJson(),
      contentType: 'application/json',
    ),
    _decodeChat,
    call: call,
  );

  static Result<Reply, Problem> _decodeChat(RawResponse r) =>
      switch (r.statusCode) {
        200 => r.success(Reply.fromJson(JsonReader.asObject(r.json))),
        400 => r.error(Problem.fromJson(JsonReader.asObject(r.json))),
        _ => r.undocumented(),
      };

  /// Taken by an older method, so the stream of chat is chatStream2.
  ///
  /// `GET /chat/stream` (`chatStream`).
  Future<Result<void, Never>> chatStream({CallOptions? call}) => _sender.send(
    OperationRequest(
      _chatStream,
    ),
    _decodeChatStream,
    call: call,
  );

  static Result<void, Never> _decodeChatStream(RawResponse r) =>
      switch (r.statusCode) {
        204 => r.success(null),
        _ => r.undocumented(),
      };

  /// Answers a prompt, whole or as it is written.
  ///
  /// Returns the events of `text/event-stream` as they arrive; the stream ends
  /// with `SseDisconnected`. It does not reconnect unless the operation's
  /// `sse.reconnect` override enables it.
  ///
  /// `POST /chat` (`chat`).
  Future<Result<Stream<SseItem<Delta>>, Problem>> chatStream2({
    required Prompt body,
    CallOptions? call,
  }) => _sender.openEvents(
    OperationRequest(
      _chatStream2,
      body: body.toJson(),
      contentType: 'application/json',
      streamStatuses: {200},
    ),
    (m) => Delta.fromJson(JsonReader.asObject(m.json)),
    _decodeChatStream2,
    call: call,
    reconnection: .off,
  );

  static Result<Stream<SseItem<Delta>>, Problem> _decodeChatStream2(RawResponse r) =>
      switch (r.statusCode) {
        400 => r.error(Problem.fromJson(JsonReader.asObject(r.json))),
        _ => r.undocumented(),
      };
}
````

### Task 5: The event union keyed by the SSE event name (R12.2)

**Files:**
- Modify: `CLAUDE.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diagnostics/diagnostic_code.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/decoder_writer.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/event_stream_writer.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/event_union_emitter.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/operation_emitter.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/api_names.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/event_data.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/event_stream_ir.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/event_streams.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/event_union_names.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/ir_operation_json.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/operation_ir.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/response_secrets.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/event_stream_emit_test.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/fixtures/chat.yaml`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/fixtures/events_hostile.yaml`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/generated_client_test.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/programs/stream_calls.dart.txt`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/event_union_test.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/ir_json_test.dart`
- Create (by `UPDATE_GOLDENS=1`): `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/goldens/chat/`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: task 3: `EventData`, `EventStreams`, `EventStreamIr`; task 4: `EventStreamWriter`, `DecoderWriter.decode`; M9: `ApiNames`, `TypeClaims`, `ResponseSecrets`, `SchemaShapes.carriesSecret` (public); `SchemaMapper.typeOf` (public).
- Produces: `EventUnionIr` (`variants`, `location`, `name`, `unknownName`, `keepUnknown`, `unknownSensitive`, `named(...)`), `EventVariantIr` (`event`, `name`, `hasData`, `data`, `sensitive`, `named(...)`), `EventStreamIr.union` and `withUnion`, `OperationIr.withTypes(events:)`, `EventShape` (`({DartType? data, EventUnionIr? union})`, what `EventData.of(media, location, prefix)` now returns), `EventUnionNames`, `EventUnionEmitter`, `ResponseSecrets.carriesSecret(Schema)`, `DiagnosticCode.eventNameDuplicate` (TOA667), `eventNameUnrepresentable` (TOA668).

Spec decision 2. An `itemSchema` whose `oneOf` (or `anyOf`) branches each fix `event` to a string `const` (or a one-value `enum`) is a union: a variant per event name whose data follows the task 3 rules (no `data`, or `data: {type: 'null'}`, is a variant without data), a repeated name keeps the first branch (TOA667), and the unknown variant's `toString` hides the data when any event's data carries a secret. A branch without a constant name makes the whole `itemSchema` text, as before (TOA666). The union is not a `SchemaMapper` declaration: `EventUnionNames` (called from `ApiNames.name`, after the M9 status types) claims `<Method>Event`, then `<Union>Unknown` (first, so an event named `unknown` is the one that gets `2`), then `<Union><Event>`; a name without an ASCII letter or digit is `<Union>Event<n>` (TOA668), a lossy one TOA559, a collision TOA551. `EventUnionEmitter` writes it after the status types: an `@immutable sealed` class whose `static fromEvent(SseMessage)` switches on `message.event` with escaped string literals, plain variant classes equal by value (`DeepEquality` for collections) with `<redacted>` for a secret, and the unknown variant `(event, data)`. The method's event decoder is `<Union>.fromEvent`. The chat fixture gets `followChat` (delta, usage via `enum`, text `note`, `done` without data); a new `events_hostile.yaml` fixture has empty, `*/`, `{@`, newline, bidi, `$`, quoted, keyword, `unknown`, `Delta`/`delta` and repeated names, recursion, a union and a secret in event data; it joins the analyzer gate, and the real calls cover an unknown event under `keep` and a decode error inside the union.

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/event_union_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diagnostics/diagnostic_code.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/ir/ir.dart';

import '../helpers/ir_fixture.dart';

/// An OpenAPI 3.2 spec whose `GET /feed` (`watch`) streams events of the
/// `itemSchema` [item] (YAML, indented under `itemSchema:`), with [schemas]
/// and, when [extra] is given, a second operation `GET /extra` returning it.
String _spec(String item, {String schemas = '{}', String? extra}) =>
    '''
openapi: 3.2.0
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
paths:
  /feed:
    get:
      operationId: watch
      responses:
        "200":
          description: ok
          content:
            text/event-stream:
              itemSchema:
$item
${extra == null ? '' : '''
  /extra:
    get:
      operationId: extra
      responses:
        "200": {description: ok, content: {application/json: {schema: $extra}}}
'''}components:
  schemas: $schemas
''';

const _delta =
    '{type: object, properties: {text: {type: string}, key: {type: string, '
    'format: password}}}';

/// The union of the only method of [fixture].
EventUnionIr _union(ClientIr ir) => ir.groups.single.operations
    .firstWhere((o) => o.name == 'watch')
    .events!
    .union!;

/// The event, class name and data of each variant of [union].
List<String> _variants(ClientIr ir, EventUnionIr union) => [
  for (final v in union.variants)
    '${v.event} ${v.name} '
        '${v.hasData ? v.data?.code(ir.nameOf) ?? 'String' : '-'}',
];

void main() {
  test('a oneOf of events with constant names is a union by event', () {
    final fixture = IrFixture(
      _spec(
        r'''
                oneOf:
                  - properties:
                      event: {const: delta}
                      data: {type: string, contentMediaType: application/json, contentSchema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Delta'}}
                  - properties: {event: {enum: [note]}, data: {type: string}}
                  - properties: {event: {const: done}}
                  - properties: {event: {const: stop}, data: {type: 'null'}}''',
        schemas: '{Delta: $_delta}',
      ),
    );
    final ir = fixture.build();
    final union = _union(ir);
    expect(union.name, 'WatchEvent');
    expect(union.unknownName, 'WatchEventUnknown');
    expect(_variants(ir, union), [
      'delta WatchEventDelta Delta',
      'note WatchEventNote String',
      'done WatchEventDone -',
      'stop WatchEventStop -',
    ]);
    expect(union.unknownSensitive, isTrue);
    expect(fixture.reportedOf(DiagnosticCode.eventItemSchemaUnread), isEmpty);
  });

  test('anyOf is read like oneOf', () {
    final fixture = IrFixture(
      _spec('''
                anyOf:
                  - properties: {event: {const: a}, data: {type: string}}
                  - properties: {event: {const: b}}'''),
    );
    final ir = fixture.build();
    expect(_variants(ir, _union(ir)), [
      'a WatchEventA String',
      'b WatchEventB -',
    ]);
  });

  test('a branch without a constant event name makes the data text', () {
    final fixture = IrFixture(
      _spec(
        '''
                oneOf:
                  - properties: {event: {const: a}, data: {type: string}}
                  - properties: {event: {type: string}, data: {type: string}}''',
      ),
    );
    final feed = fixture.build().groups.single.operations.single;
    expect(feed.events!.union, isNull);
    expect(feed.events!.data, isNull);
    expect(
      fixture.reportedOf(DiagnosticCode.eventItemSchemaUnread),
      hasLength(1),
    );
  });

  test('a repeated event name keeps the first branch (TOA667)', () {
    final fixture = IrFixture(
      _spec('''
                oneOf:
                  - properties: {event: {const: a}, data: {type: string}}
                  - properties: {event: {const: a}, data: {type: integer}}'''),
    );
    final ir = fixture.build();
    expect(_variants(ir, _union(ir)), ['a WatchEventA String']);
    expect(
      fixture
          .reportedOf(DiagnosticCode.eventNameDuplicate)
          .single
          .pointer
          ?.value,
      '#/paths/~1feed/get/responses/200/content/text~1event-stream/'
      'itemSchema/oneOf/1',
    );
  });

  test('hostile event names get safe, distinct class names', () {
    final fixture = IrFixture(
      _spec(r'''
                oneOf:
                  - {properties: {event: {const: ""}}}
                  - {properties: {event: {const: "*/"}}}
                  - {properties: {event: {const: unknown}}}
                  - {properties: {event: {const: Delta}}}
                  - {properties: {event: {const: delta}}}
                  - {properties: {event: {const: class}}}
                  - {properties: {event: {const: "a\nb"}}}'''),
    );
    final ir = fixture.build();
    expect(
      [for (final v in _union(ir).variants) v.name],
      [
        'WatchEventEvent1',
        'WatchEventEvent2',
        'WatchEventUnknown2',
        'WatchEventDelta',
        'WatchEventDelta2',
        'WatchEventClass',
        'WatchEventAB',
      ],
    );
    expect(_union(ir).unknownName, 'WatchEventUnknown');
    expect(
      fixture.reportedOf(DiagnosticCode.eventNameUnrepresentable),
      hasLength(2),
    );
    expect(fixture.reportedOf(DiagnosticCode.nameCollision), hasLength(2));
  });

  test('the union is named after the models (TOA551)', () {
    final fixture = IrFixture(
      _spec(
        '''
                oneOf:
                  - {properties: {event: {const: a}}}''',
        schemas:
            '{WatchEvent: {type: object, properties: {id: {type: string}}}}',
        extra: r"{$ref: '#/components/schemas/WatchEvent'}",
      ),
    );
    final ir = fixture.build();
    expect(_union(ir).name, 'WatchEvent2');
    expect(ir.declarations.map((d) => d.name), contains('WatchEvent'));
  });

  test('a secret in event data is a secret variant', () {
    final fixture = IrFixture(
      _spec(
        '''
                oneOf:
                  - properties: {event: {const: token}, data: {type: string, format: password}}
                  - properties: {event: {const: ping}, data: {type: string}}''',
      ),
    );
    final union = _union(fixture.build());
    expect([for (final v in union.variants) v.sensitive], [true, false]);
    expect(union.unknownSensitive, isTrue);
  });

  test('a union and recursion in event data are mapped as data', () {
    final fixture = IrFixture(
      _spec(
        r'''
                oneOf:
                  - properties:
                      event: {const: tree}
                      data: {type: string, contentMediaType: application/json, contentSchema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Node'}}
                  - properties:
                      event: {const: pet}
                      data: {type: string, contentMediaType: application/json, contentSchema: {oneOf: [{$ref: '#/components/schemas/Cat'}, {type: integer}]}}''',
        schemas:
            '{Node: {type: object, properties: {children: {type: array, '
            r"items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Node'}}}}, "
            'Cat: {type: object, properties: {name: {type: string}}}}',
      ),
    );
    final ir = fixture.build();
    expect(_variants(ir, _union(ir)), [
      'tree WatchEventTree Node',
      'pet WatchEventPet WatchEventPetData',
    ]);
  });
}
````

````diff
+    (_) => events(
+      'event: delta\ndata: {"part": "Hi", "sessionToken": "s2"}\n\n'
+      'event: usage\ndata: {"tokens": 3}\n\n'
+      'event: note\ndata: plain text\n\n'
+      'event: ping\ndata: {}\n\n'
+      'event: usage\ndata: {"tokens": "many"}\n\n'
+      'event: done\ndata: \n\n',
+    ),
+  ]);
+  final follow = await client(adapter).chat.followChat(chatId: 'c1');
+  if (follow case Success(:final data)) {
+    await for (final item in data) {
+      final shown = switch (item) {
+        SseEvent(data: FollowChatEventDelta(:final data)) => 'delta ${data.part}',
+        SseEvent(data: FollowChatEventUsage(:final data)) =>
+          'usage ${data.tokens}',
+        SseEvent(data: FollowChatEventNote(:final data)) => 'note $data',
+        SseEvent(data: FollowChatEventDone()) => 'done',
+        SseEvent(data: final FollowChatEventUnknown unknown) => '$unknown',
+        _ => describe(item),
+      };
+      print('  $shown');
+    }
+  }
+
   // Text events and an undocumented status.
   adapter = Adapter([(_) => events('data: line 1\ndata: line 2\n\n')]);
   await printAll(await client(adapter).threads.tailLog());
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/event_union_emitter.dart`:

````dart
import '../ir/dart_type.dart';
import '../ir/ir.dart';
import 'dart_code.dart';
import 'dart_writer.dart';
import 'decoder_writer.dart';
import 'type_codec.dart';

/// Writes the event union of a stream method (decision 2 of M10, R12.2):
/// a sealed class whose `fromEvent` switches on the SSE event name, a
/// variant per event that wraps its data and is equal by value, and a
/// variant for an event no variant names, as M8 unions keep an unknown
/// value.
final class EventUnionEmitter {
  /// Creates an emitter that writes with [_codec].
  EventUnionEmitter(this._codec) : _decoder = DecoderWriter(_codec);

  final TypeCodec _codec;
  final DecoderWriter _decoder;

  static const _code = DartCode();

  /// Writes [union], the events of [operation] of [group].
  void write(
    DartWriter out,
    EventUnionIr union,
    OperationIr operation,
    GroupIr group,
  ) {
    _codec.imports
      ..fromRuntime('SseMessage')
      ..fromRuntime('immutable');
    final method = '${group.name}.${operation.name}';
    _base(out, union, method);
    for (final variant in union.variants) {
      out
        ..line()
        ..prose(
          '${variant.event.isEmpty ? 'The event with an empty name' : 'The '
                    '`${_code.docSpan(variant.event)}` event'} of [$method]'
          '${variant.hasData ? '.' : ', which has no data.'}',
        )
        ..block('class ${variant.name} extends ${union.name}', () {
          if (variant.hasData) {
            _withData(out, variant);
          } else {
            _withoutData(out, variant.name);
          }
        });
    }
    out.line();
    _unknown(out, union, method);
  }

  /// Writes the sealed class with `fromEvent`.
  void _base(DartWriter out, EventUnionIr union, String method) {
    out
      ..prose(
        'The events of [$method], by their event name: a `switch` names '
        'every one.',
      )
      ..line('@immutable')
      ..block('sealed class ${union.name}', () {
        out
          ..prose('Creates the value.')
          ..line('const ${union.name}();')
          ..line()
          ..prose('Decodes [message] by its event name.')
          ..line(
            'static ${union.name} fromEvent(SseMessage message) => '
            'switch (message.event) {',
          );
        out.indented(() {
          for (final variant in union.variants) {
            out.line('${_code.string(variant.event)} => ${_value(variant)},');
          }
          out.line('_ => ${union.unknownName}(message.event, message.data),');
        });
        out.line('};');
      });
  }

  /// The variant of one event, decoded from `message`.
  String _value(EventVariantIr variant) {
    if (!variant.hasData) return 'const ${variant.name}()';
    final data = variant.data;
    final value = data == null
        ? 'message.data'
        : _decoder.decode(data, 'message.json');
    return '${variant.name}($value)';
  }

  static void _withoutData(DartWriter out, String name) {
    out
      ..prose('Creates the value.')
      ..line('const $name();')
      ..line()
      ..line('@override')
      ..line('bool operator ==(Object other) => other is $name;')
      ..line()
      ..line('@override')
      ..line('int get hashCode => ($name).hashCode;')
      ..line()
      ..line('@override')
      ..line("String toString() => '${_code.escaped(name)}()';");
  }

  void _withData(DartWriter out, EventVariantIr variant) {
    final name = variant.name;
    final type = variant.data ?? const CoreType(.string);
    final deep = _codec.needsDeep(type);
    if (deep) _codec.imports.fromRuntime('DeepEquality');
    final shown = variant.sensitive ? '<redacted>' : r'$data';
    out
      ..prose('Wraps [data].')
      ..line('const $name(this.data);')
      ..line()
      ..prose('The data of the event.')
      ..line('final ${_codec.type(type)} data;')
      ..line()
      ..line('@override')
      ..chain('bool operator ==(Object other) =>', [
        'other is $name',
        if (deep)
          'const DeepEquality().equals(other.data, data)'
        else
          'other.data == data',
      ], '&&')
      ..line()
      ..line('@override')
      ..line(
        deep
            ? 'int get hashCode => const DeepEquality().hash(data);'
            : 'int get hashCode => data.hashCode;',
      )
      ..line()
      ..line('@override')
      ..line("String toString() => '${_code.escaped(name)}($shown)';");
  }

  static void _unknown(DartWriter out, EventUnionIr union, String method) {
    final name = union.unknownName;
    final shown = union.unknownSensitive ? '<redacted>' : r'$data';
    out
      ..prose(
        'An event of [$method] whose name no other variant has, with its '
        'data as text.',
      )
      ..block('class $name extends ${union.name}', () {
        out
          ..prose('Wraps an event named [event] with [data].')
          ..line('const $name(this.event, this.data);')
          ..line()
          ..prose('The event name.')
          ..line('final String event;')
          ..line()
          ..prose('The data as received.')
          ..line('final String data;')
          ..line()
          ..line('@override')
          ..chain('bool operator ==(Object other) =>', [
            'other is $name',
            'other.event == event',
            'other.data == data',
          ], '&&')
          ..line()
          ..line('@override')
          ..line('int get hashCode => Object.hash(event, data);')
          ..line()
          ..line('@override')
          ..line(
            "String toString() => '${_code.escaped(name)}(\$event, $shown)';",
          );
      });
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/event_union_names.dart`:

````dart
import 'ir.dart';
import 'ir_context.dart';
import 'names.dart';
import 'type_claims.dart';

/// Names the event union of a stream method (decision 2 of M10) in the
/// library scope, after the declarations and the M9 status types: the
/// sealed class `<Method>Event`, its unknown variant `<Union>Unknown`, then
/// a variant per event, `<Union><Event>` (`<Union>Event<n>` for an event
/// name without an ASCII letter or digit, TOA668). The unknown variant is
/// named before the others, so an event named `unknown` is the one that
/// gets a suffix.
final class EventUnionNames {
  /// Creates the namer; it claims through [_claims].
  const EventUnionNames(this._context, this._claims);

  static const _names = DartNames();

  final IrContext _context;
  final TypeClaims _claims;

  /// The events of [operation] with their union named after [prefix]; null
  /// when the events are not a union.
  EventStreamIr? name(OperationIr operation, String prefix) {
    final events = operation.events;
    final union = events?.union;
    if (events == null || union == null) return null;
    final name = _claims.claim(
      '${prefix}Event',
      'the events of ${operation.name}',
      operation.location,
      override: operation,
    );
    final unknown = union.keepUnknown
        ? _claims.claim(
            '${name}Unknown',
            'the unknown events of ${operation.name}',
            operation.location,
            override: operation,
          )
        : '';
    return events.withUnion(
      union.named(name, unknown, [
        for (final (index, variant) in union.variants.indexed)
          variant.named(_variantName(name, variant, index, operation, union)),
      ]),
    );
  }

  String _variantName(
    String union,
    EventVariantIr variant,
    int index,
    OperationIr operation,
    EventUnionIr ir,
  ) {
    final words = _names.pascal(variant.event);
    final candidate = '$union${words ?? 'Event${index + 1}'}';
    if (words == null) {
      _context.report(
        .eventNameUnrepresentable,
        'Event "${variant.event}" has no ASCII letter or digit; its class is '
        'named after its place.',
        ir.location,
        fix: 'Name the event with letters at the source.',
      );
    } else {
      _context.checkLossyName(
        variant.event,
        words,
        ir.location,
        what: 'Event name',
      );
    }
    return _claims.claim(
      candidate,
      'the class of the event "${variant.event}"',
      operation.location,
      override: operation,
    );
  }
}
````

````diff
+  /// fix `event` to a string constant; null for any other `itemSchema`.
+  /// A repeated event name is reported (TOA667) and its later branch left
+  /// out.
+  EventUnionIr? _union(
+    SchemaObject item,
+    SpecLocation location,
+    String prefix,
+  ) {
+    final key = item.oneOf.isNotEmpty ? 'oneOf' : 'anyOf';
+    final named = _branches(item.oneOf.isNotEmpty ? item.oneOf : item.anyOf);
+    if (named == null) return null;
+    final variants = <EventVariantIr>[];
+    final seen = <String>{};
+    for (final (index, (event, branch)) in named.indexed) {
+      final at = location.child(key).child(index);
+      if (!seen.add(event)) {
+        _context.report(
+          .eventNameDuplicate,
+          'Another branch of the itemSchema is the event "$event" already; '
+          'this branch is left out.',
+          at,
+          fix: 'Give each event its own name at the source.',
+        );
+        continue;
+      }
+      variants.add(_variant(event, branch, at, prefix, index));
+    }
+    return EventUnionIr(
+      variants,
+      location: location,
+      // The data of an unknown event is text that may be any event's.
+      unknownSensitive: named.any((e) {
+        final data = _dataSchema(e.$2);
+        return data != null && _secrets.carriesSecret(data);
+      }),
+    );
+  }
+
+  /// The [branches] of a union with the event name each fixes; null when
+  /// there are none or one fixes no name.
+  List<(String, SchemaObject)>? _branches(List<Schema> branches) {
+    if (branches.isEmpty) return null;
+    final named = <(String, SchemaObject)>[];
+    for (final branch in branches) {
+      final resolved = _context.model.resolveSchema(branch);
+      final event = resolved is SchemaObject ? _eventName(resolved) : null;
+      if (resolved is! SchemaObject || event == null) return null;
+      named.add((event, resolved));
+    }
+    return named;
+  }
+
+  /// The event name a branch fixes: `event: {const: x}` or a one-value
+  /// `enum` of a string.
+  String? _eventName(SchemaObject branch) {
+    final event = branch.properties['event'];
+    final schema = event == null ? null : _context.model.resolveSchema(event);
+    if (schema is! SchemaObject) return null;
+    return switch ((schema.constValue, schema.enumValues)) {
+      ((final String name,), _) => name,
+      (null, [final String name]) => name,
+      _ => null,
+    };
+  }
+
+  /// The variant of [event], from its [branch] at [at]: no data when the
+  /// branch has no `data` or a `data` that is only `null`.
+  EventVariantIr _variant(
+    String event,
+    SchemaObject branch,
+    SpecLocation at,
+    String prefix,
+    int index,
+  ) {
+    final data = branch.properties['data'];
+    final schema = data == null ? null : _context.model.resolveSchema(data);
+    if (data == null ||
+        schema is SchemaObject &&
+            schema.types.length == 1 &&
+            schema.types.single == SchemaType.nullType) {
+      return EventVariantIr(event, hasData: false);
+    }
+    final words = _names.pascal(event) ?? 'Event${index + 1}';
+    final read = _dataOf(data, NameHint(prefix, 'Event${words}Data'));
+    if (read == null) _unread(at.child('properties').child('data'));
+    final type = read?.$1;
+    return EventVariantIr(
+      event,
+      data: type,
+      sensitive: _secrets.isSecret(
+        _dataSchema(branch),
+        type ?? const CoreType(.string),
+      ),
+    );
+  }
+
+  /// The schema of the data of an event [branch]: its `contentSchema`, else
+  /// its `data`.
+  Schema? _dataSchema(SchemaObject branch) {
+    final data = branch.properties['data'];
+    final schema = data == null ? null : _context.model.resolveSchema(data);
+    return switch (schema) {
+      SchemaObject(:final contentSchema?) => contentSchema,
+      _ => data,
+    };
+  }
+
+  void _unread(SpecLocation location) => _context.report(
+    .eventItemSchemaUnread,
+    'The itemSchema does not say what the data of an event is: it needs a '
+    '"data" property that is a string, or JSON with a "contentSchema"; the '
+    'data is read as text.',
+    location,
+    fix:
+        'Give "data" contentMediaType: application/json and a '
+        'contentSchema at the source.',
+  );
+
   /// The type of JSON data of [schema]; null when it is a plain string,
   /// which an event carries as text, not as a JSON string.
   DartType? _json(Schema schema, NameHint hint) {

+/// A sealed type of the events of a stream keyed by the SSE event name
+/// (decision 2 of M10): a variant per event and one for unknown events.
+final class EventUnionIr {
+  /// Creates the union of [variants], described at [location].
+  const EventUnionIr(
+    this.variants, {
+    required this.location,
+    this.name = '',
+    this.unknownName = '',
+    this.keepUnknown = true,
+    this.unknownSensitive = false,
+  });
+
+  /// The variants, in the order of the spec, one per event name.
+  final List<EventVariantIr> variants;
+
+  /// Where the union is described: the `itemSchema`, or the override.
+  final SpecLocation location;
+
+  /// The Dart name of the sealed class, given after the declarations.
+  final String name;
+
+  /// The class of an event no variant names; given before the variants'.
+  final String unknownName;
+
+  /// Whether an unknown event is an item of the [unknownName] variant;
+  /// otherwise it is dropped (`sse.unknown: ignore`).
+  final bool keepUnknown;
+
+  /// Whether the data of an unknown event may hold a secret, so its
+  /// `toString` does not print it: some event's data carries one.
+  final bool unknownSensitive;
+
+  /// This union with its names and [variants] named.
+  EventUnionIr named(
+    String name,
+    String unknownName,
+    List<EventVariantIr> variants,
+  ) => EventUnionIr(
+    variants,
+    location: location,
+    name: name,
+    unknownName: unknownName,
+    keepUnknown: keepUnknown,
+    unknownSensitive: unknownSensitive,
+  );
+}
+
+/// One event of an [EventUnionIr].
+final class EventVariantIr {
+  /// Creates the variant of the event named [event].
+  const EventVariantIr(
+    this.event, {
+    this.name = '',
+    this.hasData = true,
+    this.data,
+    this.sensitive = false,

+  /// The event name as the spec writes it (the SSE `event` field).

+
+  /// The Dart name of the variant's class, given after the declarations.
+  final String name;
+
+  /// Whether the event carries data; an event without data is a class
+  /// without fields.
+  final bool hasData;
+
+  /// The type the JSON data decodes to; null for text data, a `String`.
+  final DartType? data;
+
+  /// Whether the data is a secret that `toString` does not print.
+  final bool sensitive;
+
+  /// This variant named [name].
+  EventVariantIr named(String name) => EventVariantIr(
+    event,
+    name: name,
+    hasData: hasData,
+    data: data,
+    sensitive: sensitive,
+  );
````

Run from `packages/typed_openapi`: `UPDATE_GOLDENS=1 dart test test/emit/event_stream_emit_test.dart`, then `dart test test/emit/event_stream_emit_test.dart` (expected `+7: All tests passed!`). The chat goldens gain `models/usage.dart.golden` and, in `api/chat_api.dart.golden`, `followChat` and its union, which must read:

````dart
/// The events of [ChatApi.followChat], by their event name: a `switch` names
/// every one.
@immutable
sealed class FollowChatEvent {
  /// Creates the value.
  const FollowChatEvent();

  /// Decodes [message] by its event name.
  static FollowChatEvent fromEvent(SseMessage message) => switch (message.event) {
    'delta' => FollowChatEventDelta(Delta.fromJson(JsonReader.asObject(message.json))),
    'usage' => FollowChatEventUsage(Usage.fromJson(JsonReader.asObject(message.json))),
    'note' => FollowChatEventNote(message.data),
    'done' => const FollowChatEventDone(),
    _ => FollowChatEventUnknown(message.event, message.data),
  };
}

/// The `delta` event of [ChatApi.followChat].
class FollowChatEventDelta extends FollowChatEvent {
  /// Wraps [data].
  const FollowChatEventDelta(this.data);

  /// The data of the event.
  final Delta data;

  @override
  bool operator ==(Object other) =>
      other is FollowChatEventDelta &&
      other.data == data;

  @override
  int get hashCode => data.hashCode;

  @override
  String toString() => 'FollowChatEventDelta($data)';
}

/// The `usage` event of [ChatApi.followChat].
class FollowChatEventUsage extends FollowChatEvent {
  /// Wraps [data].
  const FollowChatEventUsage(this.data);

  /// The data of the event.
  final Usage data;

  @override
  bool operator ==(Object other) =>
      other is FollowChatEventUsage &&
      other.data == data;

  @override
  int get hashCode => data.hashCode;

  @override
  String toString() => 'FollowChatEventUsage($data)';
}

/// The `note` event of [ChatApi.followChat].
class FollowChatEventNote extends FollowChatEvent {
  /// Wraps [data].
  const FollowChatEventNote(this.data);

  /// The data of the event.
  final String data;

  @override
  bool operator ==(Object other) =>
      other is FollowChatEventNote &&
      other.data == data;

  @override
  int get hashCode => data.hashCode;

  @override
  String toString() => 'FollowChatEventNote($data)';
}

/// The `done` event of [ChatApi.followChat], which has no data.
class FollowChatEventDone extends FollowChatEvent {
  /// Creates the value.
  const FollowChatEventDone();

  @override
  bool operator ==(Object other) => other is FollowChatEventDone;

  @override
  int get hashCode => (FollowChatEventDone).hashCode;

  @override
  String toString() => 'FollowChatEventDone()';
}

/// An event of [ChatApi.followChat] whose name no other variant has, with its
/// data as text.
class FollowChatEventUnknown extends FollowChatEvent {
  /// Wraps an event named [event] with [data].
  const FollowChatEventUnknown(this.event, this.data);

  /// The event name.
  final String event;

  /// The data as received.
  final String data;

  @override
  bool operator ==(Object other) =>
      other is FollowChatEventUnknown &&
      other.event == event &&
      other.data == data;

  @override
  int get hashCode => Object.hash(event, data);

  @override
  String toString() => 'FollowChatEventUnknown($event, <redacted>)';
}
````

### Task 6: The `sse` override

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: task 5: `EventUnionIr`, `EventData`, `EventUnionEmitter`; M4: `OverrideLoader`, `OperationOverrideReader`, `OverrideFieldReader` (`map`, `boolean`, `checkKeys`, `invalid`), `OverrideApplier`, `OverrideEditContext` (`object`, `report`), `OverrideEdit`, `SpecEdit`, `IrOverrides.operationAt`; M7: `SseItemDecoder`.
- Produces: `SseDisconnected.lastEventId` and `retry`, `SseOverride` (`pointer`, `data`, `events`, `keepUnknown`, `reconnect`, `method`, `component(name)`), `SseMethod` (`stream`, `only`, `none`), `OperationOverride.sse`, `SseOverrideReader`, `SseOverrideApplier`, `StreamShape.excluded`, `EventUnionIr.keeping(keepUnknown:)`, `EventData.of(..., sse:)`, runtime `SseEventIgnored` (exported), `DiagnosticCode.sseOverrideNotApplied` (TOA669); `emitClient(..., overrides:)` in `test/helpers/emit_fixture.dart`.

Spec decision 6. `sse` on an operation override is read by `SseOverrideReader`: `data` or `events` (a `components.schemas` key, which becomes a `$ref` escaped as a JSON pointer and a URI, an inline schema, or for an event `null`), `unknown` (`keep` | `ignore`), `reconnect` (bool), `method` (`stream` | `only` | `none`); camelCase keys, an unknown one TOA803 with "did you mean", a bad value TOA802, `data` with `events` TOA802 and neither kept. `data` and `events` are spec fields: `SseOverrideApplier` (for the `OperationTarget` that had no edits) writes `data`, or an object whose properties are the events with data, as the `schema` of every `text/event-stream` media type of the 2xx responses written in the operation, and removes their `itemSchema`, so the override wins over both in every OpenAPI version; a `$ref`'d response is shared and is not changed, and an `sse` that finds nothing is TOA669. The rest are generation fields: `EventData` reads the union of `events` from that object in the override's order; `unknown: ignore` makes the union keep no unknown variant and `fromEvent` throw `SseEventIgnored`, which `SseItemDecoder` drops (runtime, all platforms) while its `id` still reaches `SseDisconnected.lastEventId` and so the `Last-Event-ID` of a reconnection (`SseItemDecoder.bind` moves into a private `_Decoding` class, so the allowlisted method leaves the list); `reconnect` gives `on` or `off` for any method, without TOA665; `method: only` keeps only the stream method under the operation's name, `none` keeps only the JSON method or leaves a stream-only operation out (`StreamShape.excluded`). The analyzer gate gets the chat fixture with `chat.overrides.yaml`, and the real calls an ignored event, events by name on a text stream with a default reconnect, and `method: only`.

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/sse_override_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diagnostics/diagnostic_code.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/ir/ir.dart';

import '../helpers/ir_fixture.dart';

/// A spec with a POST `chat` that offers JSON and events, a GET `feed` of
/// text events with an `itemSchema`, a GET `plain` without events and a
/// GET `shared` whose stream response is a `$ref`.
const _spec = r'''
openapi: 3.2.0
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
paths:
  /chat:
    post:
      operationId: chat
      responses:
        "200":
          description: ok
          content:
            application/json: {schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Reply'}}
            text/event-stream: {schema: {type: string}}
  /feed:
    get:
      operationId: feed
      responses:
        "200":
          description: ok
          content:
            text/event-stream:
              itemSchema: {properties: {data: {type: string}}}
  /plain:
    get:
      operationId: plain
      responses:
        "204": {description: none}
  /shared:
    get:
      operationId: shared
      responses:
        "200": {$ref: '#/components/responses/Events'}
components:
  responses:
    Events:
      description: shared
      content:
        text/event-stream: {schema: {type: string}}
  schemas:
    Reply: {type: object, properties: {text: {type: string}}}
    Delta: {type: object, properties: {part: {type: string}}}
''';

/// The methods of the client built with the overrides [overrides].
(Map<String, OperationIr>, ClientIr, IrFixture) _build(String overrides) {
  final fixture = IrFixture(_spec, overrides: overrides);
  final ir = fixture.build();
  return (
    {
      for (final group in ir.groups)
        for (final o in group.operations) o.name: o,
    },
    ir,
    fixture,
  );
}

void main() {
  test('data wins over itemSchema and schema', () {
    final (methods, ir, fixture) = _build('''
operations:
  feed: {sse: {data: Delta}}
  chat: {sse: {data: {type: object, properties: {n: {type: integer}}}}}
''');
    expect(methods['feed']!.events!.data?.code(ir.nameOf), 'Delta');
    expect(
      methods['chatStream']!.events!.data?.code(ir.nameOf),
      'ChatStreamEvent',
    );
    expect(fixture.reportedOf(DiagnosticCode.eventItemSchemaUnread), isEmpty);
  });

  test('events are a union in the order of the override', () {
    final (methods, ir, _) = _build('''
operations:
  feed: {sse: {events: {delta: Delta, done: null, note: {type: string}}}}
''');
    final union = methods['feed']!.events!.union!;
    expect(
      [
        for (final v in union.variants)
          '${v.event} ${v.name} '
              '${v.hasData ? v.data?.code(ir.nameOf) ?? 'String' : '-'}',
      ],
      [
        'delta FeedEventDelta Delta',
        'done FeedEventDone -',
        'note FeedEventNote String',
      ],
    );
    expect(union.keepUnknown, isTrue);
    expect(union.unknownName, 'FeedEventUnknown');
  });

  test('unknown: ignore drops the unknown variant', () {
    final (methods, _, _) = _build('''
operations:
  feed: {sse: {events: {a: null}, unknown: ignore}}
''');
    final union = methods['feed']!.events!.union!;
    expect(union.keepUnknown, isFalse);
    expect(union.unknownName, '');
  });

  test('reconnect turns a POST on and a GET off, without TOA665', () {
    final (methods, _, fixture) = _build('''
operations:
  chat: {sse: {reconnect: true}}
  feed: {sse: {reconnect: false}}
''');
    expect(methods['chatStream']!.events!.reconnection, StreamReconnection.on);
    expect(methods['feed']!.events!.reconnection, StreamReconnection.off);
    expect(fixture.reportedOf(DiagnosticCode.eventStreamReconnectOff), isEmpty);
  });

  test('method only keeps the stream under the operation name', () {
    final (methods, _, fixture) = _build('''
operations:
  chat: {sse: {method: only}}
''');
    expect(methods.keys, isNot(contains('chatStream')));
    expect(methods['chat']!.events, isNotNull);
    expect(fixture.reportedOf(DiagnosticCode.eventStreamMethodAdded), isEmpty);
  });

  test('method none keeps the JSON method, or leaves out a stream', () {
    final (methods, _, _) = _build('''
operations:
  chat: {sse: {method: none}}
  feed: {sse: {method: none}}
''');
    expect(methods['chat']!.events, isNull);
    expect(methods.keys, isNot(contains('chatStream')));
    expect(methods.keys, isNot(contains('feed')));
  });

  test('sse finds no event stream written in the operation (TOA669)', () {
    final (_, _, fixture) = _build('''
operations:
  plain: {sse: {reconnect: true}}
  shared: {sse: {data: Delta}}
''');
    expect(
      [
        for (final d in fixture.diagnostics.all)
          if (d.code == DiagnosticCode.sseOverrideNotApplied) d.pointer?.value,
      ],
      ['#/operations/plain/sse', '#/operations/shared/sse'],
    );
  });
}
````
