**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/overrides/sse_override_reader_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diagnostics/diagnostic_bag.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diagnostics/diagnostic_code.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/overrides/override_loader.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/overrides/sse_override.dart';

/// The `sse` field of operation `chat` in an overrides file whose `sse` is
/// [sse] (YAML), with the diagnostics in [bag].
SseOverride? _read(String sse, DiagnosticBag bag) {
  final set = OverrideLoader(
    bag,
  ).parse('operations:\n  chat:\n    sse: $sse\n', 'o.yaml')!;
  return set.operations.single.sse;
}

void main() {
  test('reads every key; a name is a reference to a component', () {
    final bag = DiagnosticBag();
    final sse = _read(
      '{data: ChatChunk, unknown: ignore, reconnect: true, method: only}',
      bag,
    )!;
    expect(bag.all, isEmpty);
    expect(sse.data, {r'$ref': '#/components/schemas/ChatChunk'});
    expect(sse.events, isNull);
    expect(sse.keepUnknown, isFalse);
    expect(sse.reconnect, isTrue);
    expect(sse.method, SseMethod.only);
    expect(sse.pointer.value, '#/operations/chat/sse');
  });

  test('events keep their order; null is an event without data', () {
    final bag = DiagnosticBag();
    final sse = _read(
      '{events: {delta: Delta, done: null, note: {type: string}}}',
      bag,
    )!;
    expect(bag.all, isEmpty);
    expect(sse.events, {
      'delta': {r'$ref': '#/components/schemas/Delta'},
      'done': null,
      'note': {'type': 'string'},
    });
  });

  test('a component key is escaped as a pointer and a URI', () {
    final bag = DiagnosticBag();
    final sse = _read("{data: 'a/b~c d%'}", bag)!;
    expect(sse.data, {r'$ref': '#/components/schemas/a~1b~0c%20d%25'});
  });

  test('data with events is an error and neither is kept', () {
    final bag = DiagnosticBag();
    final sse = _read('{data: A, events: {a: A}}', bag)!;
    expect(sse.data, isNull);
    expect(sse.events, isNull);
    expect(
      [for (final d in bag.all) (d.code, d.pointer?.value)],
      [(DiagnosticCode.invalidOverride, '#/operations/chat/sse')],
    );
  });

  test('bad values are TOA802, unknown keys TOA803 with a suggestion', () {
    final bag = DiagnosticBag();
    final sse = _read(
      '{recconect: true, method: all, unknown: drop, events: {}, data: 3}',
      bag,
    )!;
    expect(sse.method, isNull);
    expect(sse.keepUnknown, isNull);
    expect(
      [for (final d in bag.sorted) (d.code, d.pointer?.value)],
      [
        (DiagnosticCode.unknownOverrideKey, '#/operations/chat/sse/recconect'),
        (DiagnosticCode.invalidOverride, '#/operations/chat/sse/method'),
        (DiagnosticCode.invalidOverride, '#/operations/chat/sse/unknown'),
        (DiagnosticCode.invalidOverride, '#/operations/chat/sse/events'),
        (DiagnosticCode.invalidOverride, '#/operations/chat/sse/data'),
      ],
    );
    expect(bag.all.first.fix, contains('Did you mean "reconnect"?'));
  });
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/test/sse/sse_event_ignored_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';

import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi_runtime/typed_openapi_runtime.dart';

import '../helpers/fake_adapter.dart';

/// An event-stream response with [text] that then closes.
ResponseBody _stream(String text, [int status = 200]) => ResponseBody(
  Stream.value(Uint8List.fromList(utf8.encode(text))),
  status,
  headers: {
    Headers.contentTypeHeader: ['text/event-stream'],
  },
);

void main() {
  test('an event the decoder ignores is not an item', () async {
    final decoder = SseItemDecoder<String>(
      (m) => m.event == 'ping' ? throw const SseEventIgnored() : m.data,
    );
    final items = await decoder
        .bind(
          Stream.value(
            utf8.encode('data: a\n\nevent: ping\ndata: x\n\ndata: b\n\n'),
          ),
        )
        .toList();
    expect(
      [
        for (final item in items)
          switch (item) {
            SseEvent(:final data) => data,
            SseDisconnected() => 'end',
            _ => '$item',
          },
      ],
      ['a', 'b', 'end'],
    );
  });

  test('the id of an ignored event is the one a reconnection sends', () async {
    var next = 0;
    final adapter = FakeAdapter(
      (_) => [
        _stream('id: 1\ndata: a\n\nid: 2\nevent: ping\ndata: x\n\n'),
        _stream('', 204),
      ][next++],
    );
    final result = await DioRequestSender(Dio()..httpClientAdapter = adapter)
        .openEvents<String, Never>(
          const OperationRequest(
            OperationInfo(operationId: 'w', method: 'GET', pathTemplate: '/w'),
            streamStatuses: {200, 204},
          ),
          (m) => m.event == 'ping' ? throw const SseEventIgnored() : m.data,
          (r) => r.undocumented(),
          call: CallOptions(
            sse: SseOptions(reconnect: true, wait: (_) async {}),
          ),
        );
    final items = await (result as Success<Stream<SseItem<String>>, Never>).data
        .toList();
    expect(items.whereType<SseEvent<String>>().map((e) => e.data), ['a']);
    expect(items.whereType<SseReconnecting<String>>(), hasLength(1));
    expect(adapter.requests[1].headers['Last-Event-ID'], '2');
  });

  test(
    'the last item carries the id of the last event, also a dropped one',
    () async {
      final items = await SseItemDecoder<String>(
        (m) => throw const SseEventIgnored(),
      ).bind(Stream.value(utf8.encode('id: 9\ndata: x\n\n'))).toList();
      final last = items.single as SseDisconnected<String>;
      expect(last.lastEventId, '9');
    },
  );

  test('it reads as its name', () {
    expect('${const SseEventIgnored()}', 'SseEventIgnored');
  });
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/overrides/sse_override.dart`:

````dart
import '../diagnostics/json_pointer.dart';

/// Which methods an operation with an event stream gets (`sse.method`).
enum SseMethod {
  /// The stream method beside the JSON method, when there is one.
  stream,

  /// Only the stream method, named as the operation.
  only,

  /// No stream method.
  none,
}

/// The `sse` field of an operation override (decision 6 of M10): what the
/// events of its stream are, what happens to unknown events, whether it
/// reconnects and which methods it gets. [data] and [events] are spec
/// fields, written into the event-stream media types of the operation;
/// the rest are generation fields the IR builder reads.
final class SseOverride {
  /// Creates the field read at [pointer].
  const SseOverride({
    required this.pointer,
    this.data,
    this.events,
    this.keepUnknown,
    this.reconnect,
    this.method,
  });

  /// Where `sse` is in the overrides file.
  final JsonPointer pointer;

  /// The schema of every event's data, as the spec writes it: an inline
  /// schema, or `{$ref: ...}` for a `components.schemas` key.
  final Map<String, Object?>? data;

  /// The events by name, in the order of the file, each with the schema of
  /// its data as [data] has it, or null for an event without data.
  final Map<String, Map<String, Object?>?>? events;

  /// `unknown`: whether an event no name matches is kept (`keep`) or
  /// dropped (`ignore`); null when not set.
  final bool? keepUnknown;

  /// `reconnect`: whether the stream reconnects unless the call says no
  /// (`true`, also for a POST) or never (`false`).
  final bool? reconnect;

  /// `method`: which methods the operation gets.
  final SseMethod? method;

  /// The schema that refers to the `components.schemas` key [name].
  static Map<String, Object?> component(String name) {
    final pointer = JsonPointer.root
        .child('components')
        .child('schemas')
        .child(name);
    // A `$ref` is a URI: a `%` or a space in the key is percent-encoded.
    return {r'$ref': Uri(fragment: pointer.value.substring(1)).toString()};
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/overrides/sse_override_applier.dart`:

````dart
import '../model/media_type_name.dart';
import '../model/spec_node.dart';
import '../normalize/spec_edit.dart';
import 'override_edit.dart';
import 'override_edit_context.dart';
import 'override_target.dart';
import 'sse_override.dart';

/// The edits of the `sse` field of an operation override (decision 6 of
/// M10): `data` or `events` become the `schema` of every
/// `text/event-stream` media type of the operation's 2xx responses, and
/// their `itemSchema` goes, so that the override wins over both. `events`
/// is written as an object whose properties are the events with data; the
/// IR builder reads them in the override's order.
///
/// Only responses written in the operation are changed: a `$ref`'d
/// response is shared, so it is not. An `sse` field that finds no event
/// stream to apply to is TOA669.
final class SseOverrideApplier {
  /// Creates an applier that works in [_context].
  const SseOverrideApplier(this._context);

  final OverrideEditContext _context;

  /// The edits of the `sse` field of [target].
  Iterable<OverrideEdit> edits(OperationTarget target) sync* {
    final entry = target.entry;
    final sse = entry.sse;
    if (sse == null) return;
    final media = _eventStreams(target.node.location);
    if (media.isEmpty) {
      _context.report(
        .sseOverrideNotApplied,
        entry,
        'sse',
        'The operation has no 2xx response written in it whose content is '
            'text/event-stream, so "sse" changes nothing.',
        fix:
            'Remove "sse", or override a shared response through its own '
            'operation.',
      );
      return;
    }
    final schema = _schemaOf(sse);
    if (schema == null) return;
    for (final (at, raw) in media) {
      yield OverrideEdit(SpecEdit.add(at.child('schema'), schema), [
        (entry, 'sse'),
      ]);
      if (raw.containsKey('itemSchema')) {
        yield OverrideEdit(SpecEdit.remove(at.child('itemSchema')), [
          (entry, 'sse'),
        ]);
      }
    }
  }

  /// The `schema` [sse] writes: `data`, or an object whose properties are
  /// the `events` with data; null when it has neither.
  static Map<String, Object?>? _schemaOf(SseOverride sse) {
    if (sse.data case final data?) return data;
    final events = sse.events;
    if (events == null) return null;
    return {
      'type': 'object',
      'properties': {
        for (final MapEntry(:key, :value) in events.entries) key: ?value,
      },
    };
  }

  /// The event-stream media types of the 2xx responses written in the
  /// operation at [operation], with their plain objects.
  List<(SpecLocation, Map<String, Object?>)> _eventStreams(
    SpecLocation operation,
  ) {
    final responses = switch (_context.object(operation)?['responses']) {
      final Map<String, Object?> responses => responses,
      _ => const <String, Object?>{},
    };
    return [
      for (final MapEntry(key: status, value: response) in responses.entries)
        if (status.startsWith('2') && response is Map<String, Object?>)
          if (response['content'] case final Map<String, Object?> content)
            for (final MapEntry(key: type, value: media) in content.entries)
              if (MediaTypeName(type).base == 'text/event-stream' &&
                  media is Map<String, Object?>)
                (
                  operation
                      .child('responses')
                      .child(status)
                      .child('content')
                      .child(type),
                  media,
                ),
    ];
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/overrides/sse_override_reader.dart`:

````dart
import '../diagnostics/json_pointer.dart';
import 'override_field_reader.dart';
import 'sse_override.dart';

/// Reads the `sse` field of an operation override (decision 6 of M10):
/// `data` or `events`, `unknown`, `reconnect` and `method`, in camelCase;
/// an unknown key is TOA803 with the closest allowed key, a bad value
/// TOA802 and ignored.
final class SseOverrideReader {
  /// Creates a reader that works through [_fields].
  const SseOverrideReader(this._fields);

  final OverrideFieldReader _fields;

  static const _keys = {'data', 'events', 'unknown', 'reconnect', 'method'};

  /// The `sse` field of [entry] at [at]; null when absent or not a mapping.
  SseOverride? read(Map<String, Object?> entry, JsonPointer at) {
    final sse = _fields.map(entry, 'sse', at);
    if (sse == null) return null;
    final p = at.child('sse');
    _fields.checkKeys(sse, _keys, p);
    var data = sse.containsKey('data')
        ? _schema(sse['data'], p.child('data'))
        : null;
    var events = _events(sse, p);
    if (data != null && events != null) {
      _fields.invalid(
        '"data" and "events" exclude each other: one type for every event, '
        'or a type per event name; both are ignored.',
        p,
        fix: 'Keep one of them.',
      );
      data = null;
      events = null;
    }
    return SseOverride(
      pointer: p,
      data: data,
      events: events,
      keepUnknown: _unknown(sse, p),
      reconnect: _fields.boolean(sse, 'reconnect', p),
      method: _method(sse, p),
    );
  }

  /// The schema [value] at [at] names: a `components.schemas` key or an
  /// inline schema.
  Map<String, Object?>? _schema(Object? value, JsonPointer at) {
    switch (value) {
      case final String name when name.isNotEmpty:
        return SseOverride.component(name);
      case final Map<String, Object?> schema:
        return schema;
    }
    _fields.invalid(
      'An event schema is a components.schemas key or an inline schema.',
      at,
    );
    return null;
  }

  Map<String, Map<String, Object?>?>? _events(
    Map<String, Object?> sse,
    JsonPointer at,
  ) {
    if (!sse.containsKey('events')) return null;
    final value = sse['events'];
    if (value is! Map<String, Object?> || value.isEmpty) {
      _fields.invalid(
        '"events" maps event names to schemas, null for an event without '
        'data.',
        at.child('events'),
      );
      return null;
    }
    final events = <String, Map<String, Object?>?>{};
    for (final MapEntry(key: name, value: schema) in value.entries) {
      if (schema == null) {
        events[name] = null;
      } else if (_schema(schema, at.child('events').child(name))
          case final read?) {
        events[name] = read;
      }
    }
    return events;
  }

  bool? _unknown(Map<String, Object?> sse, JsonPointer at) =>
      switch (sse['unknown']) {
        null when !sse.containsKey('unknown') => null,
        'keep' => true,
        'ignore' => false,
        _ => _invalid('"unknown" must be keep or ignore.', at.child('unknown')),
      };

  SseMethod? _method(Map<String, Object?> sse, JsonPointer at) {
    if (!sse.containsKey('method')) return null;
    for (final method in SseMethod.values) {
      if (sse['method'] == method.name) return method;
    }
    return _invalid(
      '"method" must be stream, only or none.',
      at.child('method'),
    );
  }

  Null _invalid(String message, JsonPointer at) {
    _fields.invalid(message, at);
    return null;
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi_runtime/lib/src/sse/sse_event_ignored.dart`:

````dart
/// Thrown by a generated event decoder for an event the stream drops
/// (`sse.unknown: ignore` in the overrides: an event whose name no variant
/// of the event union has). `SseItemDecoder` emits no item for it, and the
/// stream goes on.
final class SseEventIgnored implements Exception {
  /// Creates the signal.
  const SseEventIgnored();

  @override
  String toString() => 'SseEventIgnored';
}
````

@@ -103,6 +56,8 @@ final class SseItemDecoder<T>
         id: message.id,
         retry: message.retry,
       );
+    } on SseEventIgnored {
+      return null;
     } on JsonDecodeException catch (e) {
       return SseDecodeError(e, message);
     } on FormatException catch (e) {
@@ -114,3 +69,83 @@ final class SseItemDecoder<T>
     }
   }
 }
+
+/// The state of one decoded stream.
+final class _Decoding<T> {
+  _Decoding(this._owner)
+    : _lastId = _owner.lastEventId,
+      _lastRetry = _owner.retry;
+
+  final SseItemDecoder<T> _owner;
+  // Set in start, before anything can listen.
+  late final StreamController<SseItem<T>> _items;
+  StreamSubscription<SseMessage>? _source;
+  bool _closed = false;
+  // What the last item carries: also the id of a dropped event.
+  String _lastId;
+  Duration? _lastRetry;
+
+  Stream<SseItem<T>> start(Stream<List<int>> stream) {
+    _items = StreamController<SseItem<T>>(
+      onListen: () => _listen(stream),
+      onPause: () => _source?.pause(),
+      onResume: () => _source?.resume(),
+      onCancel: () {
+        _close();
+        return _source?.cancel();
+      },
+    );
+    return _items.stream;
+  }
+
+  void _listen(Stream<List<int>> stream) {
+    _source = SseDecoder(lastEventId: _owner.lastEventId, retry: _owner.retry)
+        .bind(stream)
+        .listen(
+          _onMessage,
+          onError: (Object error, StackTrace stackTrace) =>
+              _end(cause: error, stackTrace: stackTrace),
+          onDone: _end,
+          cancelOnError: true,
+        );
+  }
+
+  void _onMessage(SseMessage message) {
+    _lastId = message.id;
+    _lastRetry = message.retry;
+    final SseItem<T>? item;
+    try {
+      item = _owner._item(message);
+      // ignore: avoid_catching_errors, forwarded to the listener
+    } on Error catch (error, stackTrace) {
+      _items.addError(error, stackTrace);
+      _source?.cancel();
+      _close();
+      _items.close();
+      return;
+    }
+    if (item != null) _items.add(item);
+  }
+
+  /// Ends the stream with [SseDisconnected], which carries the stream's
+  /// last event id and retry.
+  void _end({Object? cause, StackTrace? stackTrace}) {
+    _items
+      ..add(
+        SseDisconnected(
+          cause: cause,
+          stackTrace: stackTrace,
+          lastEventId: _lastId,
+          retry: _lastRetry,
+        ),
+      )
+      ..close();
+    _close();
+  }
+
+  void _close() {
+    if (_closed) return;
+    _closed = true;
+    _owner.onClose?.call();
+  }
+}

+        if (item.lastEventId case final id?) _lastEventId = id;

### Task 7: Fixes from the fuzz: stream method names of reserved words, the M10 runtime names

**Files:**
- Modify: `CLAUDE.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/config/operation_settings_reader.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/event_streams.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/names.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/config/operation_settings_test.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/event_stream_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/stream_reserved_names_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: task 3: `EventStreams.methodName`, `OperationSettingsReader.streamSuffix`; M6: `DartNames.takenTypeNames`.
- Produces: `naming.stream_suffix` must match `^[A-Z0-9][A-Za-z0-9]*$`; `SseEventIgnored`, `SseOptions`, `SseReconnecting`, `SseReconnection`, `SseReconnector`, `SseResume` are taken type names.

The M9 fuzz harness, extended with event streams and `sse` overrides (see "Fuzz"), built every spec twice with no crash and no difference, and its 500-output sample had 42 analyzer infos of one defect: an operation named `class` or `toString` gets the method `class_` (the reserved-word suffix), so its stream method was `class_Stream` and its `OperationInfo` `_class_Stream`, which are not lowerCamelCase. The stream method now takes the suffix in place of the trailing `_` (`classStream`), and `naming.stream_suffix` must start with an uppercase letter or a digit and hold letters and digits only (a suffix such as `_live` gave the same lint); a bad one is TOA902 as before. A review of the new runtime names found the second defect: a schema named `SseEventIgnored` (or another runtime type M10 adds) would have clashed with the runtime import of the generated file, so they join `DartNames.takenTypeNames` (TOA550, `Model`). After the fix the sample has 0 issues.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/stream_reserved_names_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diagnostics/diagnostic_code.dart';

import '../helpers/ir_fixture.dart';

void main() {
  test('a schema named like an M10 runtime type gets Model (TOA550)', () {
    const names = [
      'SseEventIgnored',
      'SseOptions',
      'SseReconnecting',
      'SseReconnection',
      'SseReconnector',
      'SseResume',
    ];
    final fixture = IrFixture('''
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
paths:
  /feed:
    get:
      operationId: feed
      responses:
        "200":
          description: ok
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                type: object
                properties:
${[for (final n in names) "                  ${n.toLowerCase()}: {\$ref: '#/components/schemas/$n'}"].join('\n')}
components:
  schemas:
${[for (final n in names) '    $n: {type: object, properties: {id: {type: string}}}'].join('\n')}
''');
    final ir = fixture.build();
    expect(
      [
        for (final d in ir.declarations) d.name,
      ].where((n) => n.startsWith('Sse')),
      [for (final n in names) '${n}Model'],
    );
    expect(
      fixture.reportedOf(DiagnosticCode.nameReserved),
      hasLength(names.length),
    );
  });
}
````

### Task 8: Documentation: what M10 closed, deferred and re-parked

**Files:**
- Modify: `docs/roadmap.md`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/dio_names_test.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/response_status_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: tasks 1-7.
- Produces: the roadmap's M10 plan link, "Moved from M10", and an "After M10" column in "Parked from the M9 reviews".

The roadmap rows with target M10 are closed (the M6 SSE rows, the M9 TOA654 row) or moved with a reason (the M7 `openEvents` row); a new "Moved from M10" section lists what M10 deferred. Every row of "Parked from the M9 reviews" gets an "After M10" verdict: two are handled here because they are cheap and in reach (the T10b M5 test helper gets a clear name and a doc comment, `bodyWith(String property)`; the first half of T2 minor 7 gets `test/ir/dio_names_test.dart`, which pins that `Response`, `Options` and `Headers` stay free and `Dio` gets `Model`), the rest are re-parked with the reason they still hold. The two tests pass at once: they pin M9 behaviour, so this task has no failing step.

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diagnostics/diagnostic_code.dart';

import '../helpers/ir_fixture.dart';

void main() {
  test('Response, Options and Headers stay free: the API shows only Dio', () {
    final fixture = IrFixture(r'''
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
paths:
  /a:
    get:
      operationId: read
      responses:
        "200":
          description: ok
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                type: object
                properties:
                  r: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Response'}
                  o: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Options'}
                  h: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Headers'}
                  d: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Dio'}
components:
  schemas:
    Response: {type: object, properties: {id: {type: string}}}
    Options: {type: object, properties: {id: {type: string}}}
    Headers: {type: object, properties: {id: {type: string}}}
    Dio: {type: object, properties: {id: {type: string}}}
''');
    final names = [for (final d in fixture.build().declarations) d.name];
    expect(names, containsAll(['Response', 'Options', 'Headers', 'DioModel']));
    expect(
      [
        for (final d in fixture.reportedOf(DiagnosticCode.nameReserved))
          d.pointer?.value,
      ],
      ['#/components/schemas/Dio'],
    );
  });
}
````

````diff
 ## Parked from the M9 reviews
 
 Findings of the M9 reviews that the fix tasks (10a, 10b, 10c) did not fix, with the review and the finding (`T<n>` is the review of task n; `T10b` the review of Task 10b). Everything else those reviews found is fixed.
 
-| Finding | Review | Why it is parked |
-|---|---|---|
-| A client with no operations gets a root class whose `dio` is unused and a header-only `api.dart` | T6 M7 | harmless, analyzer clean; `TOA563` already warns |
-| A tag or schema name of about 245 characters or more makes a file name over 255 and a `FileSystemException` at write (`api_library.dart`, `ModelLibrary.snake`, also models) | T4 minor 6 | no real spec has such a name; the writer should report TOA831 for it when it is met |
-| `api_client.dart` does not re-export the runtime types the API uses (`Result`, `Success`, `CallOptions`, `FileUpload`, `Date`), so a caller needs a second import | T4 minor 8 | a design decision (a re-export would make the runtime part of the client's own API) |
-| An explicit JSON `null` cannot be told from an absent value for a `JsonValue` parameter or part (an extension type over `Object?` erases): `JsonValue(null)` is "absent" for a required JSON-content parameter too | T7 minor 3 | the type cannot carry the difference; consistent with `QueryParameter('h', h)`; a wrapper type would change every signature |
-| No runtime or calls test sends an omitted optional `JsonValue` in a JSON-content or form/multipart body position | T7 minor 3 | the analyzer gate covers the shape; add with the next change there |
-| The `toString` of a status variant prints a raw JSON body with no secret in its schema in full (a free-form object may hold a token at run time) | T9 minor 1 | same rule as the model fields (M8); confirm the intent before changing it |
-| Tests for secret shapes of status bodies: a `$ref` secret, a nullable `anyOf`, a map of secrets, an envelope (all work, nothing pins them) | T9 minor 5 | test gap only |
-| A default of an array or object whose items do not fit their schema is not checked (only the `enum` and the `format` of the parameter's own schema: TOA660) | T2 minor 3 | needs a validator of values against schemas; the type mismatch is TOA211 |
-| `EncodingIr.explode` and `allowReserved` are kept for multipart although only `contentType` is used there | T2 minor 7 | harmless; the emitter reads what it needs |
-| A reserved-name test does not assert that `Response`, `Options` and `Headers` get no TOA550; tests for a default on a header, cookie or `content` parameter | T2 minor 7, 2b | test gaps; the behaviour is covered by the analyzer gate |
-| Test gaps of the naming and security work: apiKey in a query or cookie, `http basic`, `mutualTLS`, `openIdConnect`; a variant and a parts-class collision (`UploadRequest2`); a lower-case `4xx`; two servers with one URL; the `naming.client` run key | T3 M5 | tests only; the reviewer verified the behaviour |
-| The collision cases of the client name (`api`, `models`) are tested only on `ApiLibrary`, not through `generate` | T6 M5 | the layout is verified by the reviewer on 13 outputs |
-| TOA568 for a scheme without a type is reported twice, with TOA111 or TOA112 at the same place | T10a M4 | each says something else (the spec is wrong; the metadata is `unknown`) |
-| `UndocumentedStatus.rawBody` is raw bytes, not parsed JSON, in an operation with a binary error (the operation reads bytes) | T10b M1 | stated in the decoder rules of `CLAUDE.md`; a parse by `Content-Type` can be added to `RawResponse.undocumented` |
-| `body(String schema)` in `response_status_test.dart` reads like an unused parameter | T10b M5 | style only |
-| The new emitter code uses `!` where a pattern or an early return could promote the value (`decoder_writer.dart`, `request_writer.dart`, `operation_emitter.dart`, `api_library.dart`) | final review minor 9 | the values are IR-contract invariants and 54,000 fuzzed builds found no crash; style only |
-| A multipart `encoding.contentType` with a control character inside a quoted parameter or a line break between parameters (`text/plain; x="a\nb"`) passes the generator's check (`ir/part_media_type.dart`) and Dio's `DioMediaType.parse` throws at call time | final re-review R1 | the media type is invalid per RFC 9110; tighten the pattern to RFC 9110 tokens and quoted strings and report TOA661 with the fallback; first item of the next change there |
-| A `security` field that is not a list (`security: {bearer: []}`) is dropped with TOA111 only, so the metadata reads as open unless the document has its own requirement (`parse/operation_parser.dart`, `parse/openapi_parser.dart`) | final re-review R2 | an invalid spec that is already a warning; map it to one alternative of kind `unknown` with TOA568, like a malformed entry |
-| `default: "2020-13-01T00:00:00Z"` on a `date-time` parameter gets no TOA660, because `DateTime.tryParse` normalises the month | final re-review R3 | check the RFC 3339 fields by range instead of `tryParse` |
````

## Corpus run

`generate` on the 14 public corpus clients after task 4, after task 7 and on the final tree on top of `fecc57c` (configs and outputs in the scratchpad with absolute `output` paths, one package per `union` mode with `dio` and the runtime by path, `dart pub get --offline`, `dart analyze --fatal-infos` under `package:lints/recommended` plus the `flutter_lints` rules); the runs give the same numbers (TOA665 aside, see below):

| | `union: raw` (default) | `union: structural` |
|---|---|---|
| Clients generated | 13 of 14 (artemis skipped: its two TOA009 `!!timestamp` errors, as in M9) | 13 of 14 |
| Files | 10,670 (stripe 6,445, openai 2,109, discord 353, jellyfin 346, medusa 338, grace 265, grace_bundle 263, airflow 203, ditto 137, googlemaps 104, tambo 99, efgs 5, digitalocean 3) | 11,668 |
| `dart analyze --fatal-infos` | 0 errors, 0 warnings, 0 infos | 0 errors, 0 warnings, 0 infos |
| Second `generate` | 0 files written | 0 files written |

Event streams: TOA654 went from 4 (M9) to **0**: every event stream of the corpus is generated. openai has 11 operations that offer JSON and `text/event-stream` (`createChatCompletion` and `createChatCompletionStream`, `createResponse`/`createResponseStream`, ...; TOA663 x11) and tambo 2 stream-only operations; all 13 are POST and none reconnects; the corpus config does not ask for reconnection, so there is no TOA665 (it was 13 before the user decision). No TOA664, TOA666, TOA667, TOA668 or TOA669: no corpus spec uses `itemSchema` or an array event schema, so the event union is exercised by the fixtures and the fuzz. openai grew from 1,948 to 2,109 files (the stream methods' event data models), tambo from 89 to 99. TOA651 went from 57 to 48: an event-stream alternative of a success is no longer reported as dropped when a stream method returns it (TOA651 stays where the JSON method drops another type, such as audio next to JSON and events).

## Fuzz

The M9 harness (`scratchpad/m9t7/fuzz`, copied to `scratchpad/m10plan/fuzz`) extended with event streams: `text/event-stream` media types with a `schema`, an array `schema`, no schema, an `itemSchema` with `data`/`contentSchema` (a component, a union, a secret, `null`, a non-string), `itemSchema` `oneOf` unions whose event names are constants, one-value enums or (to break the union) plain strings, from a hostile set (`""`, `*/`, `{@x}`, a newline, a bidi control, `$cost`, `it's`, `class`, `unknown`, `Delta`/`delta`, `message`, `toString`, `fromEvent`, `hashCode`), JSON next to an event stream, operationIds that collide with stream names (`get0Stream`, `get0`), OpenAPI 3.2 next to 3.0 and 3.1, and an `sse` override on a third of the operations (`events` with hostile names and `null`, `data` as a key or inline, `unknown`, `reconnect`, `method`). Each spec is built twice and `ApiLibrary.files` compared: 6,000 seeds x 3 configurations x 2 `union` modes = 36,000 builds, 0 crashes, 0 non-deterministic outputs, 324,465 files. The fuzz was run again on the final tree on top of `fecc57c`, with the same result. The sample of 500 outputs (every 12th seed, one configuration, `raw` mode; 251 files with `openEvents`, 96 with an event union, 5 with `SseEventIgnored`, 8 reconnecting by default) in one package with the analyzer gate's rules: the first run found 42 infos of one defect (task 7), after the fix 0.

## Deferred

Written into the roadmap's "Moved from M10" (task 8):

- NDJSON/JSONL streams (R12.6), WebSockets, webhooks and callbacks (spec, "Out of scope").
- The `sse` override does not change a `$ref`'d (shared) response (TOA669 when it finds nothing else).
- `sse.method: none` on an operation that is only an event stream leaves it out, and the TOA563 message counts it among the event streams the client cannot express.
- A per-call `reconnect: true` on a POST is ignored at run time by design (the method's doc comment says so, and TOA665 when the config asks to reconnect).
- An event stream on the web still needs a streaming adapter the user plugs into Dio (M7, spec decision 8).

## Self-review

Spec decisions: 1 - tasks 3, 6 (`EventData`: `itemSchema.data`/`contentSchema`, text `data`, `schema` as the data, array items with TOA664; the override wins over both by spec edits). 2 - task 5 (union keyed by the event name, `fromEvent`, a variant without data for `null`, the unknown variant `(event, data)`, M8 shape, names after the models), task 6 (`events`). 3 - task 1 (`SseOptions`, `SseReconnection`, `Last-Event-ID`, 1 s doubling to 30 s, 5 attempts, all configurable), task 3 (per operation by config, TOA665), task 6 (per operation by override, also for a POST), task 4 (`reconnection:` argument). 4 - task 1 (`SseReconnecting` before each attempt, `SseDisconnected` last, no errors). 5 - tasks 3-4 (two methods, `naming.stream_suffix`, TOA663, no body field guessed), task 6 (`method`), task 7 (reserved words); the name override is the operation's `name` (open question 2). 6 - task 6 (every key, camelCase, TOA803 with "did you mean", `data` with `events` an error). 7 - tasks 3-4 (named arguments, `body:`, `CallOptions? call`, `Future<Result<Stream<SseItem<T>>, E>>`, the stream opens for documented successes only, other statuses decode as in M9, `SseDecodeError` and the stream goes on). 8 - task 4 (browser test). 9 - tasks 3, 5, 6 (TOA654 narrowed, TOA663-TOA669). Gates: goldens, the analyzer gate and real calls (tasks 4-6), runtime on VM, dart2js and dart2wasm (tasks 1, 4, 6), corpus in both modes and the fuzz (above). No placeholders; names and signatures between tasks were checked by the replay from `main`.

## Open questions

Each is a user-facing choice the spec leaves open; the plan implements the default given. The user confirmed 1-7, 9-11, 13 and 14 as they are; 8 and 12 are decided.

1. **`sse.reconnect` in the config applies to GET operations only**; a POST reconnects only by the `sse.reconnect` override of that operation. Alternative: the config key also enables POST streams.
2. **The second method's name** is `<name><stream_suffix>`, where `<name>` follows the operation's `name` override; there is no separate key for the stream method's name. Alternative: an `sse.name` key in the override.
3. **A `schema` of plain `type: string`** (and no schema) makes the data text, `String`, not a JSON string to decode. Alternative: decode `"..."` as JSON.
4. **`itemSchema.data` with a `contentSchema` but no `contentMediaType`** is text with TOA666 (strict). Alternative: take it as JSON.
5. **A server `retry` longer than `maxDelay`** is kept; only the doubling stops at `maxDelay`. Alternative: cap every delay.
6. **The attempt count starts again after an event** (or a decode error) on a new connection. Alternative: count every attempt of the call.
7. **An attempt answered with an error status ends the stream** (`SseDisconnected(cause: HttpError ...)`) instead of trying again; a transport failure is tried again. Alternative: retry 5xx too.
8. **Decided (user):** TOA665 only when the config's `sse.reconnect` is on, once per non-GET event-stream operation the override does not enable, with the override as the fix; no diagnostic otherwise (0 on the corpus). Every non-GET stream method's doc comment says it does not reconnect unless the operation's `sse.reconnect` override enables it.
9. **Names:** the union is `<Method>Event` (`FollowChatEvent`), its variants `<Union><Event>` (`FollowChatEventDelta`), the unknown variant `<Union>Unknown`, inline data of a variant `<Union><Event>Data`, inline data of a single-type stream `<Method>Event`; an event name without letters or digits gives `<Union>Event<n>` (TOA668).
10. **`SseReconnecting` joins the sealed `SseItem`**, so existing exhaustive switches over `SseItem` in user code need a new case (a breaking change of the runtime, which is not published yet).
11. **The `sse` override's `data`/`events` are applied to responses written in the operation only**; a shared (`$ref`'d) response is not changed (TOA669 when nothing else is). Alternative: change the shared response with TOA808, as other response overrides do.
12. **Decided (controller ruling):** `unknown: ignore` drops the item, but its `id` still moves the `Last-Event-ID` a reconnection sends (`SseDisconnected.lastEventId`), as WHATWG keeps the last event id for any dispatched event.
13. **`naming.stream_suffix` must start with an uppercase letter or a digit** (letters and digits only), so the stream method stays lowerCamelCase; `_live` is TOA902. Alternative: allow any identifier part and let the analyzer warn.
14. **`sse.method: none` on a stream-only operation leaves it out** without a diagnostic of its own (the user asked for it). Alternative: an info that names it.
